  - 88:18 **ٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B004: A hot penetrating wind reinforces heat as something that passes through and invades.

## marking liquid
- reading: The administered liquid can be imagined as a dye-like medium that marks its recipients from within.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:5 **تُسْقَىٰ** س ق ي B009: Soaking cloth with dye supplies liquid that changes and marks what absorbs it.
  - 88:5 **عَيْنٍ** ع ي ن B010: Cloud and lasting rain keep the marking analogy within a water-bearing domain.
  - 88:18 **ٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B003: First rain that marks the earth with vegetation supplies visible inscription by water.


# Focus 88:6

## exclusive ration
- reading: They occupy a closed present ration in which the whole category of food is denied and one plant-source alone is admitted.
- mechanism: Present-state negation closes the recipients' food field; the exception does not diversify that field but reduces it to the plant named by ضريع.
- trace:
  - 88:6 **لَّيْسَ** ل ي س B001: Fixed present-state negation supplies the clause's closed current condition.
  - 88:6 **لَّيْسَ** ل ي س B003: Categorical negation makes the restriction stronger than a casual statement of scarcity.
  - 88:6 **طَعَامٌ** ط ع م B001: Taste and intake supply the ordinary food function that the clause first raises and then constricts.
  - 88:6 **ضَرِيعٍ** ض ر ع B005: The named plant supplies the sole concrete item left inside the closed ration.

## provision of need
- reading: The verse identifies their entire allotted subsistence as a provision that embodies need and returns weakness instead of relief.
- mechanism: The sole allotted provision is not merely a substance associated with deprivation; its branch field embodies the recipient's need and bodily depletion, making provision reproduce want.
- trace:
  - 88:6 **طَعَامٌ** ط ع م B004: Provision, livelihood, and allotted share widen food into the recipients' assigned means of subsistence.
  - 88:6 **ضَرِيعٍ** ض ر ع B002: Needful submission turns the allotted provision into an enacted posture of dependence.
  - 88:6 **ضَرِيعٍ** ض ر ع B003: Weakness and emaciation supply the bodily result that this anti-provision carries within itself.

## threshold meal
- reading: The exception can carry an anti-meal suspended near readiness, with completion continually approached but nutritional arrival denied.
- mechanism: Two readiness images converge on a meal held at the threshold of completion: it approaches flavor and doneness, yet the negative-exclusive frame never lets readiness become nourishing availability.
- trace:
  - 88:6 **لَّيْسَ** ل ي س B001: Present negation blocks the passage from anticipated readiness to available food.
  - 88:6 **طَعَامٌ** ط ع م B005: Ripening and taking on flavor supply one trajectory toward edible completion.
  - 88:6 **ضَرِيعٍ** ض ر ع B007: A pot nearing doneness supplies a second, still-incomplete trajectory toward a meal.

## category slippage
- reading: The sole exception may only grammatically restore food while materially slipping into a thin, marginal intake.
- mechanism: The sole exception slides toward the edge of the category it is supposed to restore: what remains after 'no food' may be only a thin drink, so grammatical exception and nutritional exception diverge.
- trace:
  - 88:6 **لَّيْسَ** ل ي س B002: Exception and exclusion supply the formal opening through which the marginal item enters.
  - 88:6 **طَعَامٌ** ط ع م B001: The intake branch admits drink only insofar as it functions as tasted nourishment.
  - 88:6 **ضَرِيعٍ** ض ر ع B011: The thin-drink image pushes the only exception toward the boundary of food rather than its center.

## event scale closure
- reading: Their closed provision is one way an encompassing event takes hold of them.
- mechanism: Arrival, event-emergence, and envelopment enlarge the baseline restriction from a private menu fact into one operative channel of an encompassing condition.
- trace:
  - 88:6 **لَّيْسَ** ل ي س B001: Present-state denial supplies the local closure that the opening event can scale up.
  - 88:6 **طَعَامٌ** ط ع م B004: Provision and livelihood make food a system-level condition rather than only an object.
  - 88:1 **أَتَىٰكَ** ء ت ي B001: Coming and arrival give the restrictive state an eventive onset.
  - 88:1 **حَدِيثُ** ح د ث B001: Something coming to be turns static lack into a condition that has occurred.
  - 88:1 **ٱلْغَٰشِيَةِ** غ ش و B002: An all-enveloping cover supplies the scale within which the closed ration operates.

## provision of need feedforward
- reading: The ration is a self-maintaining metabolic trap: labor empties the body, intake returns emaciation, and hunger remains.
- mechanism: The sequence begins with embodied submission and exhausting work, then supplies a ration whose own branch field is emaciation, and finally denies fattening and hunger-relief. Intake therefore feeds the state of depletion rather than interrupting it.
- trace:
  - 88:6 **طَعَامٌ** ط ع م B001: Ordinary intake supplies the expected metabolic intervention that the mechanism reverses.
  - 88:6 **ضَرِيعٍ** ض ر ع B003: Weakness and emaciation place the body's depleted output inside the ration itself.
  - 88:2 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B004: The face standing for the person makes the deprivation visibly embodied.
  - 88:2 **خَٰشِعَةٌ** خ ش ع B001: Lowered submission aligns the recipients' posture with the needful field of ضريع.
  - 88:3 **عَامِلَةٌ** ع م ل B001: Purposeful labor supplies the energy expenditure preceding the ration.
  - 88:3 **نَّاصِبَةٌ** ن ص ب B004: Wearisome fatigue supplies the depleted state that food would normally repair.
  - 88:7 **يُسْمِنُ** س م ن B001: Fatness as the contrary of wasting names one expected repair and explicitly removes it.
  - 88:7 **يُغْنِى** غ ن ي B002: Sufficiency names the functional threshold that this ration cannot meet.
  - 88:7 **جُوعٍ** ج و ع B001: The empty belly remains as the unchanged output after intake.

## labor return reversal
- reading: طعام also behaves like the allotted return on labor, and ضريع is the humiliating anti-wage paid to exhausted work.
- mechanism: The provision sense of طعام allows two labor-return sequences to be compared: exhausting work receives a deprivation-allotment, while purposeful striving elsewhere receives satisfaction. The focus becomes an anti-wage as well as an anti-meal.
- trace:
  - 88:6 **طَعَامٌ** ط ع م B004: Livelihood, earnings, and allotted share turn food into a possible return for labor.
  - 88:6 **ضَرِيعٍ** ض ر ع B002: Need and submission characterize the return assigned to the first laboring group.
  - 88:3 **عَامِلَةٌ** ع م ل B004: Wage and worker-provision expose a compensation frame inside the earlier labor.
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B002: Work and earning supply the comparison term for a second kind of effort.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B001: Satisfaction rather than resentment supplies the contrasting outcome of that effort.

## threshold meal heated
- reading: The ration is an engineered anti-meal: processing reaches heat and maturity, but what is completed is harm rather than nourishment.
- mechanism: Fire, active heat, administered drink, and arrival at full maturity activate the focus's ripening and near-done-pot branches. The ration can be read as a deliberately processed anti-meal whose thermal completion intensifies while nutritional completion fails.
- trace:
  - 88:6 **طَعَامٌ** ط ع م B005: Ripening and flavor supply the focus-side expectation that processing should culminate in edible value.
  - 88:6 **ضَرِيعٍ** ض ر ع B007: The pot nearing doneness anchors the processed-meal image directly in ضريع.
  - 88:4 **تَصْلَىٰ** ص ل ي B003: Encountering fire supplies the heating environment around the focus ration.
  - 88:4 **نَارًا** ن و ر B002: Blazing fire intensifies processing from generic warmth to active combustion.
  - 88:4 **حَامِيَةً** ح م ي B001: Heating names the thermal operation that links fire to the near-done vessel.
  - 88:5 **تُسْقَىٰ** س ق ي B001: Administered drinking moves the heated material from environment toward bodily intake.
  - 88:5 **ءَانِيَةٍ** ء ن ي B003: Reaching full maturity supplies the completion point that the food process reaches only as harmful intensity.

## exclusive ration function cancelled
- reading: The exception is a category trap: it occupies the name 'food' while reinstating 'no food' at the level of bodily function.
- mechanism: The follow-up tests the sole exception against two core food functions and cancels both. ضريع remains grammatically inside the food slot while operating as food's functional negation.
- trace:
  - 88:6 **لَّيْسَ** ل ي س B003: Categorical negation provides the outer denial that the failed exception effectively reinstates.
  - 88:6 **طَعَامٌ** ط ع م B001: Food as tasting, eating, and satisfying hunger supplies the prototype placed under test.
  - 88:6 **ضَرِيعٍ** ض ر ع B003: Emaciation makes the sole food resemble the very failure food should reverse.
  - 88:7 **يُسْمِنُ** س م ن B001: The gain opposed to emaciation is explicitly denied as an output.
  - 88:7 **يُغْنِى** غ ن ي B002: Sufficiency supplies a functional criterion that the exception fails.
  - 88:7 **جُوعٍ** ج و ع B001: Bodily emptiness persists, canceling the exception's claim to satisfy.

## hospitality relation denied
- reading: The problem also lies in a failed giver-recipient relation: the only offer degrades, while the contrasting scene makes provision flow and stand ready for use.
- mechanism: The positive scene distributes provision through flow, vessels, deliberate placement, alignment, and spread. Focus branches expose feeding and offering as relations between giver and recipient, so the negative ration becomes a collapse of hospitality, not merely a bad ingredient.
- trace:
  - 88:6 **طَعَامٌ** ط ع م B002: Feeding another and asking to be fed make nourishment an interpersonal delivery relation.
  - 88:6 **ضَرِيعٍ** ض ر ع B009: Offering property turns the sole item into a degraded gift or allocation.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B001: Good condition supplies the recipient-state achieved by the contrasting provision system.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B006: Continuous provision supplies an uninterrupted delivery channel.
  - 88:14 **وَأَكْوَابٌ** ك و ب B001: The drinking vessel gives provision a usable recipient-facing form.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B001: Placement in an appointed position supplies deliberate readiness for use.
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: Orderly alignment supplies coordinated service rather than random availability.
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B001: Distribution and spreading complete the image of provision arranged around recipients.

## pastoral circuit collapsed
- reading: ضريع is the collapsed remainder of an entire pastoral provision circuit: plant and udder remain as signs, while growth-to-milk nourishment has failed.
- mechanism: The later material sequence can be assembled into a pastoral nourishment circuit: measured creation, sky-borne rain, fertile spreading earth, plants, tended camels, and milk rising in udders. The focus compresses that productive circuit into a dry plant and the lexical trace of an udder, with nourishment absent.
- trace:
  - 88:6 **طَعَامٌ** ط ع م B004: Provision and livelihood provide the output expected from the reconstructed ecological circuit.
  - 88:6 **ضَرِيعٍ** ض ر ع B001: Udder and milk anchor the circuit's animal nourishment endpoint inside the focus word.
  - 88:6 **ضَرِيعٍ** ض ر ع B005: The dry named plant anchors the circuit's collapsed vegetal residue inside the same focus word.
  - 88:17 **ٱلْإِبِلِ** ء ب ل B001: Camels and their tending supply the herd that can convert forage into nourishment.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B001: Measure and proportion supply the designed fit among the circuit's material parts.
  - 88:18 **ٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B003: The non-dominant mapped image of first rain marking earth with growth supplies water-to-vegetation activation.
  - 88:18 **رُفِعَتْ** ر ف ع B007: Milk rising in the udder explicitly links the raised-sky context back to the milk-bearing focus branch.
  - 88:20 **ٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B002: Soft fertile earth supplies the ground in which nourishment-bearing growth should occur.
  - 88:20 **سُطِحَتْ** س ط ح B006: A plant spreading over the ground completes the forage side of the circuit.

## human feeder agency weakened
- reading: The announcing human role is bounded to reminder rather than control, so administration of the ration must be located elsewhere or left unstated.
- mechanism: The later repetition of present negation denies controlling oversight to the reminder. This limits a reading in which the speaking reminder personally administers or coerces the focus ration, while leaving the ration's coercive structure intact.
- trace:
  - 88:6 **لَّيْسَ** ل ي س B001: Present-state negation supplies the repeated form whose second use reallocates agency.
  - 88:6 **طَعَامٌ** ط ع م B002: Feeding another exposes the possible feeder role that the later agency boundary constrains.
  - 88:21 **فَذَكِّرْ مُذَكِّرٌ** ذ ك ر B009: Reminder and reminder-object define the human addressee's stated function.
  - 88:22 **لَّسْتَ** ل ي س B001: The repeated present denial excludes control from that reminder's current role.
  - 88:22 **بِمُصَيْطِرٍ** س ط ر B003: Dominating oversight names precisely the administrative agency being denied.

## refusal returned as ration
- reading: The ration can coexist with a reciprocal reading: covered provision returns as an allotment that manifests need rather than relieving it.
- mechanism: Turning away and covering blessing can be read reciprocally against food-as-provision: refusal and concealment return as a provision whose sole content is need and wasting.
- trace:
  - 88:6 **طَعَامٌ** ط ع م B004: Provision and livelihood supply the good that can be covered and then inverted.
  - 88:6 **ضَرِيعٍ** ض ر ع B002: Need and submission supply the reciprocal form taken by the inverted provision.
  - 88:6 **ضَرِيعٍ** ض ر ع B003: Wasting supplies the bodily consequence of provision reversed into deprivation.
  - 88:23 **تَوَلَّىٰ** و ل ي B007: Turning away supplies the initiating relation of refusal.
  - 88:23 **وَكَفَرَ** ك ف ر B004: Covering a blessing supplies the specific object of refusal that the ration can invert.

## punishment as weaning
- reading: Punishment also operates as weaning from nourishment itself: intake persists, but sweetness, sufficiency, and bodily repair are withheld.
- mechanism: The later punishment root carries sweetness of food and water, bodily abstention from intake, withholding or weaning, and inflicted pain. Those branches gather the focus into a deprivation process: punishment is not only what is eaten but the removal of sweetness, appetite, and nourishment through eating.
- trace:
  - 88:6 **طَعَامٌ** ط ع م B001: Taste, food, and hunger-relief supply the nourishing functions subjected to punitive reversal.
  - 88:6 **ضَرِيعٍ** ض ر ع B003: Emaciation supplies the body produced when nourishment is withheld in the act of intake.
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B001: Sweetness and goodness in water or food name the positive sensory value removed from the ration.
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B002: The body's abstention from food and drink turns punishment into blocked intake or appetite.
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B003: Withholding and weaning supply the directional operation that deprives while food remains present.
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B005: Painful punishment identifies the juridical force into which the food mechanism is gathered.

## accounted allotment
- reading: The clause can read like an accounted allotment: their sole recorded share is an anti-provision that is counted yet cannot suffice.
- mechanism: Return followed by reckoning activates the possessive and restrictive grammar of the focus as an allocation statement. Food-as-allotted share becomes a counted entitlement whose sole entry is precisely what cannot suffice.
- trace:
  - 88:6 **لَّيْسَ** ل ي س B002: Exclusion reduces the recipients' account to one admissible entry.
  - 88:6 **طَعَامٌ** ط ع م B004: Allotted share, revenue, and livelihood make food legible as an assigned entitlement.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return to the place of return supplies the transition into final allocation.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B001: Counting and accounting supply the formal operation that fixes the ration's entry.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B003: Sufficiency supplies an ironic accounting criterion because the allotted food has already failed it.

## throat harness
- reading: The food briefly activates as an intake apparatus: a strand-like ration driven toward a throat that it grips rather than feeds.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:6 **طَعَامٌ** ط ع م B012: Gripping and squeezing the throat converts the food pathway into a site of restraint.
  - 88:6 **ضَرِيعٍ** ض ر ع B013: Rope strands supply the material image of a harness or constricting line.
  - 88:5 **تُسْقَىٰ** س ق ي B001: The non-dominant mapped image of driving or herding supplies coercive motion toward intake.

## withheld milk
- reading: The word also carries a lactation-negative echo: the organ and rising of milk are activated, yet the sole ration yields no milk-like nourishment.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:6 **لَّيْسَ** ل ي س B001: Negation supplies the withholding frame around the expected nourishing yield.
  - 88:6 **ضَرِيعٍ** ض ر ع B001: Udder and milk place a source of animal nourishment inside the sole food's branch field.
  - 88:17 **ٱلْإِبِلِ** ء ب ل B001: Camels and their tending supply the animal body to which the udder image belongs.
  - 88:18 **رُفِعَتْ** ر ف ع B007: Milk rising in the udder supplies an exact latent yield that the focus's negative ration withholds.

## thin drink exception
- reading: The surviving item can hover between food and thin drink, so the exception restores a grammatical item without securely restoring nourishment.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:6 **لَّيْسَ** ل ي س B002: Exception supplies the narrow gate through which a category-edge item enters.
  - 88:6 **طَعَامٌ** ط ع م B001: Taste and nourishing intake allow drink to count as food only by function.
  - 88:6 **ضَرِيعٍ** ض ر ع B011: The thin-drink image supplies the marginal substance that destabilizes the exception's category.
  - 88:5 **تُسْقَىٰ** س ق ي B001: Giving drink activates the beverage side immediately before the food clause.
  - 88:14 **وَأَكْوَابٌ** ك و ب B001: The cup supplies a contrasting vessel in which drink is usable and recipient-ready.

## sunset ration
- reading: The ration can carry a terminal temporal echo: nourishment is replaced by perpetual approach to setting and return.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:6 **ضَرِيعٍ** ض ر ع B006: The sun nearing its setting turns the ration into an image of terminal approach.
  - 88:18 **ٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B004: The sky as the elevated cover supplies the spatial field of the sunset image.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B007: Sunset returning to its resting place links terminal light to the surah's final return.


# Focus 88:7

## two stage nutritional failure
- reading: It fails both conversion into bodily reserve and immediate cancellation of bodily lack: intake occurs, but neither accumulation nor relief follows.
- mechanism: The substance fails two different nutritional tests: it adds no bodily reserve and it does not functionally suffice to terminate an existing deficit. The second clause is not merely a synonym of the first; a thing might fail to fatten yet still relieve hunger, but this does neither.
- trace:
  - 88:7 **يُسْمِنُ** س م ن B001: Bodily fatness, opposite leanness, supplies the accretion pole blocked by the first negation.
  - 88:7 **يُغْنِى** غ ن ي B002: Sufficing, benefiting, or standing in supplies the functional-relief pole blocked by the second negation.
  - 88:7 **جُوعٍ** ج و ع B001: The empty stomach and its pain specify the bodily deficit that remains operative.

## hollow surplus
- reading: It can also expose hollow enlargement: nothing in this intake produces real substance, independence, or an answer to inner lack.
- mechanism: An apparent increase can be socially or rhetorically inflated without producing real independence. The two negations puncture that false surplus: it cannot enlarge standing and cannot release the subject from an inward lack figured as hunger or longing.
- trace:
  - 88:7 **يُسْمِنُ** س م ن B010: Self-inflating claims to unowned merit or rank supply the deceptive outward increase.
  - 88:7 **يُغْنِى** غ ن ي B001: Wealth and independence from need supply the genuine sufficiency that the apparent increase cannot create.
  - 88:7 **جُوعٍ** ج و ع B004: Hunger extended to emptiness or longing lets the unresolved lack exceed a merely caloric reading.

## failed scarcity settlement
- reading: In a scarcity frame, the ration neither repairs a deficient share nor counts as an adequate substitute; private hunger and failed distribution coexist.
- mechanism: Under scarcity, provision has distributive as well as bodily work: it should repair a short share or substitute adequately for what is missing. The coordinated negation allows neither function, so the item leaves both the allocation and the need uncorrected.
- trace:
  - 88:7 **يُسْمِنُ** س م ن B008: Equalizing partners' shares by returning excess supplies a distributive correction that the first negation can withhold.
  - 88:7 **يُغْنِى** غ ن ي B002: Sufficing or standing in for something supplies the standard for an adequate replacement.
  - 88:7 **جُوعٍ** ج و ع B002: A famine-time expands the deficit from one stomach to a scarcity regime in which shares matter.

## labor return failure
- reading: It is the unusable return on exhausting work: the body pays the cost, but the ration neither rebuilds the worker nor clears the hunger-debt.
- mechanism: The focus food becomes a failed return on exertion. Work consumes the body and conventionally yields wage or provision, but here the exhausted worker receives an intake that restores neither mass nor satiety; the later contrasting group is satisfied with its striving, showing a successful work-to-outcome circuit.
- trace:
  - 88:7 **يُسْمِنُ** س م ن B001: Fattening supplies the bodily restoration that exhausted labor fails to receive.
  - 88:7 **يُغْنِى** غ ن ي B002: Sufficing and benefiting supply the expected utility of a worker's provision.
  - 88:7 **جُوعٍ** ج و ع B001: Empty-stomach pain is the metabolic debt left after exertion and intake.
  - 88:3 **عَامِلَةٌ** ع م ل B004: Work's wage or worker's provision supplies the exchange link between exertion and the food that should repay it.
  - 88:3 **نَّاصِبَةٌ** ن ص ب B004: Exhaustion that wears a person down supplies the bodily cost requiring replenishment.
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B002: Work and earnings supply the contrasting circuit in which striving has an intelligible yield.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B001: Satisfaction rather than displeasure marks the successful outcome missing from the exhausted group's circuit.

## thermal counterfunction
- reading: Within the heat sequence it may also fail to cool: neither thermal stress nor hunger finds an effective countermeasure.
- mechanism: The rare cooling branch of س م ن becomes live after a fire-and-drink sequence. In that thermal field, the first negation can deny cooling relief while the second still denies nutritional relief, making the substance fail against two simultaneous bodily stresses.
- trace:
  - 88:7 **يُسْمِنُ** س م ن B003: The exceptional sense of cooling supplies the surprising counterfunction blocked by لا يُسْمِنُ.
  - 88:7 **يُغْنِى** غ ن ي B002: Availing or benefiting keeps the second denial as a broad failure of relief.
  - 88:7 **جُوعٍ** ج و ع B001: Hunger supplies the concurrent deficit that remains after thermal relief also fails.
  - 88:4 **تَصْلَىٰ** ص ل ي B003: Meeting fire and its heat establishes direct thermal exposure immediately before the focus.
  - 88:4 **حَامِيَةً** ح م ي B001: Heat and heating intensify the condition for which cooling would be a relevant function.
  - 88:5 **تُسْقَىٰ** س ق ي B001: Giving drink supplies the expected relief-channel, which the sequence renders hostile rather than restorative.

## darii nutritional inversion
- reading: This is anti-nourishment: a substance occupying the food slot and carrying latent fat-and-milk expectations converts intake into neither body nor relief, while its own root field yields emaciation.
- mechanism: The named food is not merely nutrient-poor; its root field stages an inversion. Food carries an image of animal fat, while ضريع carries coexisting images of plant, udder-milk, and emaciation. Expected nourishment is lexically present but its outcome is reversed into leanness, explaining why intake produces neither reserve nor relief.
- trace:
  - 88:7 **يُسْمِنُ** س م ن B001: Fatness opposite leanness supplies the expected somatic result explicitly denied.
  - 88:7 **يُغْنِى** غ ن ي B002: Sufficing and benefiting supply the food-function that the inversion defeats.
  - 88:7 **جُوعٍ** ج و ع B001: The empty stomach supplies the persistent endpoint despite the presence of something called food.
  - 88:6 **طَعَامٌ** ط ع م B007: Animal fat and the taste of grease make the label 'food' carry precisely the fattening expectation denied next.
  - 88:6 **ضَرِيعٍ** ض ر ع B001: Udder and milk supply a latent image of nourishment inside the name of the exclusive food.
  - 88:6 **ضَرِيعٍ** ض ر ع B003: Weakness and emaciation reverse the udder-milk expectation and converge on the negated fattening.
  - 88:6 **ضَرِيعٍ** ض ر ع B005: The named plant keeps the activation anchored to the immediate substance rather than replacing it with the remote root images.

## interrupted provision regime
- reading: The ration belongs to an interrupted provision regime: supply is present at the mouth but cannot flow onward into bodily reserve, sufficiency, or settled well-being.
- mechanism: The opposite scene supplies a complete provision cycle: good condition is visible, giving continues, and vessels enable drinking. Against that cycle, 88:7 is not only a statement about one food's composition but the bottleneck of an interrupted regime in which intake never becomes durable well-being.
- trace:
  - 88:7 **يُسْمِنُ** س م ن B001: Bodily increase supplies the missing conversion from provision into durable condition.
  - 88:7 **يُغْنِى** غ ن ي B002: Sufficing and benefiting supply the failed endpoint of the hostile provision regime.
  - 88:7 **جُوعٍ** ج و ع B001: Hunger is the unresolved demand that distinguishes mere availability from successful provision.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B001: Good condition and beneficence supply the visible outcome of a successful provision cycle.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B006: Continuous provision and giving turn the running source into a sustained, rather than one-off, supply mechanism.
  - 88:14 **وَأَكْوَابٌ** ك و ب B005: Drinking from a cup supplies the usable delivery endpoint present in the contrasting scene.

## failed body making
- reading: It fails as body-making material: unlike forms configured for solidity and endurance, this intake cannot become reserve or confer resilience against scarcity.
- mechanism: The observational sequence activates a body-making analogy. Camel care and making do without water, complete proportioned formation, and mountain-like thickness all display robust form or stored capacity; the focus substance does the reverse, entering a body without generating reserve, resilience, or relief.
- trace:
  - 88:7 **يُسْمِنُ** س م ن B001: Fattening supplies the conversion of intake into embodied reserve that fails in the focus.
  - 88:7 **يُغْنِى** غ ن ي B002: Sufficing supplies resilience against need, the functional result absent alongside bodily reserve.
  - 88:7 **جُوعٍ** ج و ع B001: Empty-stomach hunger supplies the scarcity pressure under which reserve should matter.
  - 88:17 **ٱلْإِبِلِ** ء ب ل B001: Camels and their care supply a concrete animal body against which nourishment and reserve can be imagined.
  - 88:17 **ٱلْإِبِلِ** ء ب ل B002: Making do without water supplies a model of embodied capacity enduring scarcity.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B003: Complete, proportioned formation supplies the successful body-making outcome opposed to wasting.
  - 88:19 **ٱلْجِبَالِ** ج ب ل B003: Thickness of bodily form lets mountain mass reinforce the image of substantial embodiment.

## punitive withholding
- reading: Its non-nutrition may be the operative punishment: intake is allowed, but transfer into body and relief is withheld so hunger continues.
- mechanism: The later punishment root carries abstention, withholding, and pain together. It changes the focus from accidental dietary inadequacy into a possible technique of sustained deprivation: something is administered as food, yet nourishment and release are withheld so that need itself persists as part of the pain.
- trace:
  - 88:7 **يُسْمِنُ** س م ن B001: Blocked bodily increase supplies the nourishment deliberately absent from the administered substance.
  - 88:7 **يُغْنِى** غ ن ي B002: Blocked benefit or sufficiency supplies the withheld release from the condition.
  - 88:7 **جُوعٍ** ج و ع B001: Persistent empty-stomach pain becomes part of the punitive mechanism rather than a side effect.
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B002: The body's abstention from food and drink supplies deprivation as a bodily state.
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B003: Withholding, prevention, or weaning supplies the active non-transfer of nourishment.
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B005: Pain and punishment supply the governing function into which sustained hunger can be recruited.

## final accounting settlement
- reading: It enters no balancing credit against the deficit; return and final accounting, not the ration, become the locus where unequal shares and claims are settled.
- mechanism: Return followed by accounting reactivates the focus branch of equalizing partners' shares. The bad ration supplies no balancing credit and no adequate offset against scarcity; the final sequence relocates settlement to a later return and reckoning, where sufficiency and accounting occupy the same root field.
- trace:
  - 88:7 **يُسْمِنُ** س م ن B008: Returning excess to equalize unequal shares supplies the settlement model denied to the ration.
  - 88:7 **يُغْنِى** غ ن ي B002: Sufficing or replacing supplies the criterion for whether the ration can count as an offset.
  - 88:7 **جُوعٍ** ج و ع B002: Famine-time supplies the scarcity ledger in which deficient shares and adequate offsets matter.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return to the destination supplies the temporal reopening of an unsettled account.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B001: Counting and accounting supply the explicit settlement procedure.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B003: Sufficiency and enrichment bridge the final account back to the denied يُغْنِى function.

## unsounded song
- reading: As a contained root echo, it also gives no sustaining voice or song to answer hunger-as-longing; the later good scene excludes both song-like audition and noise.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:7 **يُغْنِى** غ ن ي B003: Song and vocal modulation supply a latent acoustic value under the focus root.
  - 88:7 **جُوعٍ** ج و ع B004: Hunger as longing supplies an affective lack to which voice or song might otherwise answer.
  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B007: Pleasurable hearing and song activate the acoustic branch without erasing the focus form.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B003: Mixed vocal noise supplies the rejected acoustic counterfield in the contrasting scene.

## surface decoration without nourishment
- reading: At the material-surface edge of the root, it cannot even furnish or color a convincing exterior; arranged appearance and internal sufficiency remain radically separate.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:7 **يُسْمِنُ** س م ن B006: Decorative pigments supply a surface treatment that cannot become bodily substance.
  - 88:7 **يُغْنِى** غ ن ي B002: Failure to suffice prevents cosmetic surface from counting as functional nourishment.
  - 88:7 **جُوعٍ** ج و ع B004: Emptiness beneath appearance supplies the interior deficit left untouched by decoration.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B001: Putting each object in a settled place begins the contrasting scene's deliberate surface arrangement.
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: Alignment on an even line supplies ordered visual presentation.
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B001: Spreading things out completes the furnishing field that activates decoration as a contrast.

## withheld milk circuit
- reading: A pastoral root network stages nourishment as present but retained: udder and milk appear, transfer closes, emaciation follows, and the eater gains neither reserve nor relief.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:7 **يُسْمِنُ** س م ن B001: Fattening supplies the bodily outcome that successful milk transfer would ordinarily support.
  - 88:7 **يُغْنِى** غ ن ي B002: Sufficing supplies the functional endpoint absent when nourishment does not transfer.
  - 88:7 **جُوعٍ** ج و ع B001: Persistent hunger supplies evidence that the latent nourishment never reaches the eater.
  - 88:6 **ضَرِيعٍ** ض ر ع B001: Udder and milk place potential animal nourishment inside the root field of the exclusive food.
  - 88:6 **ضَرِيعٍ** ض ر ع B003: Weakness and emaciation show the bodily result when that nourishment fails to transfer.
  - 88:13 **مَّرْفُوعَةٌ** ر ف ع B007: Milk retained in the udder supplies the hidden valve that blocks transfer, though the source surface immediately describes raised couches.
  - 88:17 **ٱلْإِبِلِ** ء ب ل B001: Camels and their care complete the pastoral body-and-milk environment of the latent circuit.


# Focus 88:8

## visible ease
- reading: The faces visibly carry softened, unstrained ease; bodily appearance and lived condition coincide.
- mechanism: The predicate transfers both tactile softness and an unstrained mode of life onto the visible front, making condition legible as countenance.
- trace:
  - 88:8 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B001: The bodily face and outward front provide the visible surface on which the state appears.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B002: Softness and easeful living supply a state that is simultaneously material, bodily, and experiential.

## whole person flourishing
- reading: Whole persons, shown through their faces, stand in a condition of received bounty and flourishing.
- mechanism: Face-for-self expands the adjective beyond skin or expression: the whole person is presented through the part that meets others, while bounty names the person's encompassing condition.
- trace:
  - 88:8 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B004: Face as self makes the visible countenance a compact reference to the whole person.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B001: Bounty and good condition extend the predicate from appearance to the person's total flourishing.

## approved orientation
- reading: Their very facing or course is excellent and rightly ordered, with comfort as one consequence of that orientation.
- mechanism: The clause can carry a directional layer in which the subjects' facings are not only comfortable but commendable and sound.
- trace:
  - 88:8 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B002: Orientation and destination turn the plural faces into plural ways of facing or proceeding.
  - 88:8 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B008: The proper aspect or sound course gives the orientation a normative rather than merely spatial value.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B003: The praise branch supplies approval, allowing the predicate to commend how these faces are directed.

## envelopment made visible
- reading: These faces are the exposed readout of how an all-covering event has met particular persons, sharply reversing the lowered faces of the paired panel.
- mechanism: An event that covers all is rendered locally readable on exposed fronts: the repeated face-clause divides the covered field into visibly opposed conditions.
- trace:
  - 88:8 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B001: The outward face is the observer-facing display surface for the focus condition.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B002: Soft ease supplies the positive condition displayed on that surface.
  - 88:1 **ٱلْغَٰشِيَةِ** غ ش و B002: The branch of a covering that encompasses everything supplies the common field within which outcomes become visible.
  - 88:2 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B001: The repeated outward face establishes the same diagnostic surface on the contrasting side.
  - 88:2 **خَٰشِعَةٌ** خ ش ع B001: Lowering and submission provide the negative visible posture against which focus ease is differentiated.

## relief from failed nourishment
- reading: The softness is relief from futile expenditure: these faces register a condition in which effort and provision no longer consume the person without answering need.
- mechanism: The negative panel depicts expenditure without return: labor becomes exhaustion and intake neither builds the body nor answers hunger. Against that failed economy, ناعمة names successful sufficiency and release from attritional need.
- trace:
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B001: Good condition and bounty supply the positive outcome absent from the failed economy.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B002: Easeful living supplies relief from strain rather than a merely decorative softness.
  - 88:3 **عَامِلَةٌ** ع م ل B001: Purposive work supplies the expenditure side of the negative mechanism.
  - 88:3 **نَّاصِبَةٌ** ن ص ب B004: Exhausting toil makes the work bodily depleting rather than fruitful.
  - 88:7 **يُسْمِنُ** س م ن B001: Bodily fattening, explicitly negated in the source clause, supplies the missing nutritive result.
  - 88:7 **يُغْنِى** غ ن ي B002: Sufficiency, also negated, identifies the deeper failure of the available intake.
  - 88:7 **جُوعٍ** ج و ع B001: The empty belly supplies the unmet need that remains after eating.

## ease reconciled with striving
- reading: The face shows post-striving reconciliation: the whole person can inhabit prior effort without regret, strain, or unresolved demand.
- mechanism: The immediate sequel prevents ease from collapsing into idleness: purposeful movement and earning are retrospectively accepted, and that settled relation to one's striving is what the face displays.
- trace:
  - 88:8 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B004: Face-for-person lets the satisfaction concern the whole agent rather than an isolated expression.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B001: Good condition supplies the settled outcome of the agent's relation to prior striving.
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B002: Work, earning, and conduct supply the purposeful history behind the present condition.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B001: Satisfaction opposed to displeasure supplies settled approval of that history.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B001: The second mapped acceptance branch preserves the split root while reinforcing willing reception rather than resentment.

## elevated dignity
- reading: They are countenances of restored dignity: persons visibly raised in standing as well as placed amid elevated surroundings.
- mechanism: Physical elevation in the positive setting activates the social-rank branch of وجه, so the focus faces can be honored persons whose ease includes restored standing.
- trace:
  - 88:8 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B006: Eminence and rank let faces denote persons made socially prominent or honored.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B001: Bounty makes the elevation beneficent rather than domineering.
  - 88:10 **عَالِيَةٍ** ع ل و B002: Elevation as honor converts the garden's height into a possible status signal.
  - 88:13 **مَّرْفُوعَةٌ** ر ف ع B002: Raised degree reinforces dignity alongside the literal raising of places of repose.

## habitat person fit
- reading: Ease is relational: their faces show how completely the surrounding place fits them, with provision flowing and the material world already arranged to answer embodied need.
- mechanism: The positive environment is not an inventory of luxuries but a fitted habitat: enclosure, acoustic quiet, continuous provision, and objects already placed, aligned, and spread remove friction between person and place.
- trace:
  - 88:8 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B004: Face-for-self supplies the person whose condition is shaped by the surrounding habitat.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B011: A place agreeing with and pleasing its inhabitant supplies the relational core of the model.
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B003: A garden enclosed by growth supplies protective habitation rather than bare exposure.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B003: Mixed clamor, explicitly absent in the source clause, supplies acoustic friction removed from the habitat.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B006: Continuous provision turns the running spring into sustained availability.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B001: Placement in an appointed usable position makes the cups ready to meet need.
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: Even alignment supplies order and accessibility rather than random accumulation.
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B001: Spreading and distribution extend usable comfort across the setting.

## inner joy becomes countenance
- reading: The countenance is the broadcast surface of inward joy, especially an eye-gladdened ease that can no longer remain hidden.
- mechanism: A latent eye, concealed joy, and disclosure sequence lets the face function as the place where inward ease comes outward: the countenance is eye-gladdened and broadcasts what had been interior.
- trace:
  - 88:8 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B001: The outward face provides the surface across which an inward state becomes visible.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B013: The formula of an eye gladdened by blessing supplies a specifically ocular form of manifest joy.
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B001: The eye branch, latent in the spring word, reactivates the focus inventory's eye-gladdening image.
  - 88:13 **سُرُرٌ** س ر ر B010: Hidden joy and ease supply the interior affect that the countenance can disclose.
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B002: Expressing what was concealed supplies the outward arrow from inward joy to visible face.

## created readable surface
- reading: The softened face is a constructed, inspectable sign: its surface makes an otherwise hidden condition readable, just as the later sequence asks visible forms to be read through how they were made.
- mechanism: The repeated inspection of made and extended forms turns surfaces into evidence. Under that activation, the focus face is not just expressive but a formed, readable sign whose softness shows the condition impressed upon it.
- trace:
  - 88:8 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B001: The front that meets an observer supplies a human surface available for inspection.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B002: Smooth softness supplies the perceivable quality carried by the formed surface.
  - 88:17 **يَنظُرُونَ** ن ظ ر B001: Directed sight and reflection supply the act of treating visible form as evidence.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B003: Completed and proportioned formation supplies a designed-form analogy for the focus countenance.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B008: A smooth, even surface materially links created form to the softness branch of ناعمة.
  - 88:18 **ٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B002: The non-dominant mapped branch supplies a visible mark by which an observer reads condition.
  - 88:20 **سُطِحَتْ** س ط ح B001: An extended even surface reinforces surface itself as a bearer of legible arrangement.

## open orientation to blessing
- reading: Their ease also signals orientation: they face rather than turn away, leave blessing uncovered, and arrive at return and account with an open countenance.
- mechanism: Reminder, face-turning, covering blessing, return, and reckoning create a trajectory of orientation. The focus faces can therefore be open, rightly directed countenances that carry acknowledged bounty rather than turning from and covering it.
- trace:
  - 88:8 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B002: Direction and destination make the focus face a trajectory as well as a body surface.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B001: Blessing and good condition supply what can be received openly or concealed.
  - 88:21 **فَذَكِّرْ مُذَكِّرٌ** ذ ك ر B003: Recollection after forgetfulness supplies a possible reorientation toward what is true.
  - 88:23 **تَوَلَّىٰ** و ل ي B006: Turning the face and approaching provide the positive directional possibility latent inside the source root.
  - 88:23 **تَوَلَّىٰ** و ل ي B007: Turning away supplies the explicit contrary trajectory against which open facing is read.
  - 88:23 **وَكَفَرَ** ك ف ر B004: Covering a blessing creates a direct negative counterpart to the bounty branch of ناعمة.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return to a destination gives every orientation a terminal point.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B001: Counting and account make the result of the trajectory answerable rather than accidental.

## affirmative countenance
- reading: The countenances also carry an embodied yes: they remain present to the address, visibly confirming where the contrasting response turns away.
- mechanism: The answer-yes branch of نعم becomes live when the closing address sets reminder against refusal and turning away. The focus face can then be response-bearing: an embodied yes shown face-to-face rather than merely spoken.
- trace:
  - 88:8 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B003: Face-to-face encounter supplies the relational scene in which a response is visibly borne.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B004: Yes, confirmation, and promise supply an affirmative response latent beneath the adjective.
  - 88:21 **فَذَكِّرْ مُذَكِّرٌ** ذ ك ر B009: Reminder supplies the address to which affirmation or refusal can answer.
  - 88:23 **تَوَلَّىٰ** و ل ي B007: Turning away supplies the bodily contrary of remaining face-to-face in response.
  - 88:23 **وَكَفَرَ** ك ف ر B005: Disavowal supplies the contrary speech-act to confirmation.

## livestock camel echo
- reading: At the edge of the reading, their ease also carries a pastoral register of tended life, dependable provision, and abundance, later reawakened by the camel.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B005: The livestock and camel branch supplies a latent pastoral register inside the focus adjective.
  - 88:17 **ٱلْإِبِلِ** ء ب ل B001: The explicit camel and its care reactivate the focus root's otherwise remote livestock register.
  - 88:17 **يَنظُرُونَ** ن ظ ر B001: Directed observation makes the camel image available as a deliberate retrospective cue rather than incidental vocabulary.

## aimlessness resolved into fit
- reading: A coexisting counter-current reads their ease as aimlessness finally resolved into a fitting orientation and inhabitable destination.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:8 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B002: Direction and destination supply the route whose disorder or resolution can change the face's state.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B011: A place fitting its inhabitant supplies the resolved destination.
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B002: The non-dominant branch of neglect or going off without orientation supplies a competing history of aimlessness.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B001: Acceptance supplies the transition from unsettled movement to a destination one can inhabit willingly.

## soft wind front
- reading: As a contained material analogy, ease moves across these outward fronts like a soft moisture-bearing current and participates in the flowing, spread habitat.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:8 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B001: Face as outward front supplies a surface across which an atmosphere can be imagined as moving.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B009: The soft moist south wind supplies dynamic gentleness rather than static texture.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B001: Flow and free movement activate the wind branch's dynamic quality.
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B001: Spreading and diffusion give the gentle condition an environmental reach.


# Focus 88:9

## earned acceptance
- reading: The subject accepts her own concrete work and earnings as worthwhile; the striving itself has become satisfactory rather than wasted.
- mechanism: سعي as work and earning supplies something performed and owned; رضا as acceptance then functions as an appraisal of that work rather than undirected pleasure.
- trace:
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B002: Work, earning, and active endeavor make the possessed سعي the concrete performance being appraised.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B001: Acceptance opposed to displeasure supplies the favorable verdict on that performance.

## telic motion
- reading: Her striving is purposeful movement that has reached what it sought, and satisfaction is its arrival condition.
- mechanism: The movement branch gives سعي a desired destination, and the approval branch lets رضا mark the achieved end-state of that directed passage.
- trace:
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B001: Purposeful going toward a sought object turns the striving into a directed trajectory.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B002: Abundant or sought approval supplies the trajectory's desired terminus.

## reconciliatory striving
- reading: Her striving may have made peace or carried generosity, so her satisfaction includes restored consent between parties.
- mechanism: سعي can be an undertaking of generosity or peacemaking, while رضا can be mutual consent; together they support satisfaction as a repaired social relation produced by her effort.
- trace:
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B006: Noble generosity and peacemaking supply a socially reparative kind of striving.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B003: Mutual consent supplies the concord that the reparative effort brings about.

## entrusted agency
- reading: Her striving can be the responsible execution of a charge, with satisfaction arising from having answered for it faithfully.
- mechanism: The possessed striving can be read as discharge of an entrusted office over others; satisfaction follows from faithful, answerable performance of that charge.
- trace:
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B003: Agency or office over a group supplies an entrusted social task for the striving.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B006: Obedience, love, or guarantee supplies faithful answerability within that entrusted task.

## face as public verdict
- reading: Her accepted striving has become a verdict written on the face: inward acceptance is outwardly and bodily evident under the common event.
- mechanism: The all-enveloping event polarizes visible faces into lowered and softened conditions. The feminine-singular راضية can continue that facial subject, making satisfaction with striving an embodied, publicly legible verdict and not merely hidden emotion.
- trace:
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B002: Concrete work remains the conduct whose result becomes visible.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B001: Acceptance supplies the inward state that the face exteriorizes.
  - 88:1 **ٱلْغَٰشِيَةِ** غ ش و B002: An event that envelops everyone supplies the common exposure under which opposed outcomes become legible.
  - 88:2 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B001: The literal face or front supplies the visible bearer of the first condition.
  - 88:2 **خَٰشِعَةٌ** خ ش ع B001: Lowering and submission give the negative face a bodily posture.
  - 88:8 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B004: Face standing for the whole self lets visible expression carry a total personal verdict.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B002: Softness and ease supply the positive face's perceptible condition immediately before the focus.

## efficacy not exhaustion
- reading: Her satisfaction answers the fruitfulness of her striving: unlike exhausting labor that leaves need intact, this endeavor reaches a fitting result.
- mechanism: The preceding negative sequence separates quantity of labor from efficacy: work can exhaust yet fail to nourish or suffice. Against that failure, رضا for one's سعي becomes satisfaction with effective, outcome-bearing endeavor, not admiration of exertion as such.
- trace:
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B002: Work and earning supply the positive sequence's own term for active endeavor.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B001: Acceptance supplies the favorable assessment that futile labor lacks.
  - 88:3 **عَامِلَةٌ** ع م ل B001: Purposeful action supplies a close comparator for the focus's striving.
  - 88:3 **نَّاصِبَةٌ** ن ص ب B004: Exhausting trouble marks labor whose intensity does not secure a good outcome.
  - 88:7 **يُغْنِى** غ ن ي B002: Sufficiency supplies the explicit criterion that the negative provision fails.
  - 88:7 **جُوعٍ** ج و ع B004: Emptiness and wasting supply the unresolved need left by ineffective provision.

## rest in prepared order
- reading: She is pleased because work can give way to repose inside a prepared order where provision now flows and the habitat does the sustaining.
- mechanism: After the focus, an enclosed garden contains provision that flows continuously and furnishings already raised, placed, aligned, and spread. This changes رضا for prior سعي into permission to cease producing and receive from an order that is already sustaining.
- trace:
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B002: Active work supplies the labor whose mode changes in the prepared setting.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B001: Acceptance supplies the transition from producing to receiving.
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B003: A garden sheltered by its trees supplies the containing habitat for the result.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B006: Continuing provision and giving make sustenance ongoing without renewed exertion by the beneficiary.
  - 88:13 **سُرُرٌ** س ر ر B011: A place of settled reclining supplies repose as the new bodily mode.
  - 88:13 **مَّرْفُوعَةٌ** ر ف ع B001: Already elevated furnishings supply completed preparation.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B001: Objects placed in their appointed location supply readiness for use.
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: Alignment on an even line supplies deliberate order rather than unfinished setup.
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B001: Spreading things throughout the space supplies complete availability across the habitat.

## burden relocated to structure
- reading: Satisfactory striving culminates when burden no longer consumes the worker and stability has been built into the world around her.
- mechanism: The same context root first images a worker worn down by naṣab and later a mountain made to stand by naṣb. The focus sits between those poles, allowing satisfactory striving to mark a transfer from carrying instability in the body to inhabiting a world whose weight has been set into stable structure.
- trace:
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B002: Work and endeavor supply the activity whose burden is being reconfigured.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B001: Acceptance marks the subject's release from the first pole of exhausting labor.
  - 88:3 **نَّاصِبَةٌ** ن ص ب B004: Wearisome affliction locates naṣb inside the laboring body.
  - 88:19 **نُصِبَتْ** ن ص ب B001: Erecting something as a prominent stable object relocates naṣb into the world's structure.

## agent world fit
- reading: Her satisfaction registers a fit: purposeful agency succeeded because creature, capacity, route, and world answered one another.
- mechanism: The inspection sequence presents an adapted traveler inside a vertically and horizontally ordered field: an enduring camel, measured aptitude, elevated sky, solid mountains, lower ground, and an extended surface. Purposeful سعي is therefore not isolated willpower but successful coupling between agent and affordant world.
- trace:
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B001: Purposeful going supplies the locomotor action whose conditions the creation sequence can expose.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B001: Acceptance supplies felt consonance between the moving agent and the fitted field.
  - 88:17 **يَنظُرُونَ** ن ظ ر B001: Directed sight or insight turns the sequence into inspection of how the mechanism works.
  - 88:17 **ٱلْإِبِلِ** ء ب ل B002: The camel's ability to manage without water supplies an organism fitted for sustained passage.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B005: Aptitude and preparedness supply the creature-to-task fit rather than bare existence.
  - 88:18 **ٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B001: Elevation supplies the upper coordinate of the traversed field.
  - 88:19 **ٱلْجِبَالِ** ج ب ل B001: Solid elevated masses supply stable structure within that field.
  - 88:20 **ٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B001: The lower realm opposed to sky supplies the ground coordinate.
  - 88:20 **سُطِحَتْ** س ط ح B001: A level extended surface supplies the direct affordance for purposeful movement.

## bounded noncoercive agency
- reading: If the office resonance remains, satisfaction belongs to faithful reminder and service without accusation or domination; the striving stays answerable but not coercive.
- mechanism: The focus inventory permits office over people and denunciation to authority, but the context excludes corrupt speech and defines the communicative office as reminder rather than controlling oversight. The agency reading survives only as responsible, noncoercive service.
- trace:
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B003: Office over people supplies the social-agency baseline that the context constrains.
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B004: Denunciation to authority supplies the coercive or accusatory failure mode of that agency.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B006: Obedience, love, or guarantee preserves answerable service after domination is removed.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B002: False or ugly speech excludes tale-bearing from the satisfactory communicative order.
  - 88:21 **فَذَكِّرْ مُذَكِّرٌ** ذ ك ر B009: Reminder as that which makes another remember supplies a bounded positive office.
  - 88:22 **بِمُصَيْطِرٍ** س ط ر B003: The controlling overseer supplies the role explicitly denied to the addressee.

## arrival return circuit
- reading: Her striving is the outbound portion of a course that finds its true completion in return, and satisfaction is arrival after directed movement.
- mechanism: The context opens with coming and closes with return, placing the focus's purposeful movement inside an arrival-return circuit. Satisfaction becomes the closure of a trajectory rather than a static feeling attached to a ledger of deeds.
- trace:
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B001: Purposeful going supplies the directed middle movement of the circuit.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B001: Acceptance supplies the settled state when that movement closes.
  - 88:1 **أَتَىٰكَ** ء ت ي B001: Coming or arrival supplies the opening directional pole.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return to a homeward place supplies the closing directional pole.

## account ratified satisfaction
- reading: Her satisfaction can be a concordant verdict: she accepts her striving because return and account have also ratified it.
- mechanism: The closing movement to divine return and accounting prevents رضا from being self-certifying. The reciprocal-consent branch allows a coexisting reading in which her acceptance of her striving and the final evaluator's acceptance converge.
- trace:
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B002: Work and earning supply the owned conduct that can be brought into account.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B003: Mutual consent supplies concord between the subject's satisfaction and an answering approval.
  - 88:24 **ٱللَّهُ** ء ل ه B001: The worshiped deity supplies the personal evaluator named before return and account.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return to the destination brings the worker and her conduct before that evaluator.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B001: Counting and accounting supply the external reckoning against which satisfaction is tested.

## double prevailing
- reading: A faint double-root resonance makes satisfaction the composure after a striving has prevailed in contest.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B008: Rivaling and prevailing in a striving supplies the contest's active phase.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B005: Overcoming another in the matter supplies the same contest's resolved outcome.

## wandering gathered into return
- reading: Satisfaction marks movement rescued from loose wandering: what could have dispersed across the open field is gathered into a homeward return.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B002: Neglected wandering supplies the risk that movement disperses without a governing end.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B001: Acceptance supplies settlement after the risk of aimless dispersal.
  - 88:17 **ٱلْإِبِلِ** ء ب ل B001: Camels and their tending make governed versus untended movement materially imaginable.
  - 88:20 **سُطِحَتْ** س ط ح B001: An extended surface supplies the open field across which motion could either stray or stay directed.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return to a homeward place gathers open-ended movement into a terminus.

## manumission ledger
- reading: Her work can image earnings that remove a claim upon her, so satisfaction is release from bondage after the account is discharged.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B005: Earning toward the price of freedom supplies a legally measured striving for release.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B001: Acceptance supplies the settled state after the claim has been discharged.
  - 88:23 **تَوَلَّىٰ** و ل ي B005: A relation involving kinship, manumission, or clientage keeps the release image socially structured.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B001: Counting and accounting supply the ledger by which the remaining claim reaches zero.


# Focus 88:10

## topographic orchard
- reading: A canopied orchard-like enclosure situated high or rising above its surroundings.
- mechanism: A tree-covered enclosure is located on, or itself forms, elevated terrain.
- trace:
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B003: The covered orchard supplies the concrete enclosure that serves as the adjective's locus.
  - 88:10 **عَالِيَةٍ** ع ل و B001: Physical rising supplies direct vertical elevation to the enclosed garden.

## sheltered high rank
- reading: A protected interior distinguished by high worth and dignity, whether or not altitude is foregrounded.
- mechanism: The place encloses and protects, while its highness marks qualitative dignity rather than only altitude.
- trace:
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B001: Concealment supplies an interior kept from exposure and ordinary perception.
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B008: Protective covering turns enclosure into refuge rather than mere visual hiding.
  - 88:10 **عَالِيَةٍ** ع ل و B002: Noble rank makes elevation a quality of the abode and its occupants.

## self rising growth
- reading: A garden whose thick, towering life makes it high from within.
- mechanism: The enclosure is high because its own dense growth thrusts upward; elevation is generated internally by vegetation rather than supplied only by external terrain.
- trace:
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B011: Vigorous interwoven growth supplies a living mass that can produce height.
  - 88:10 **عَالِيَةٍ** ع ل و B010: Tall, large-bodied form gives the vegetation a vertically expansive build.

## covering reversed into refuge
- reading: The upward, inward reversal of an overwhelming world: exhausted exposure is exchanged for protective elevation and satisfied arrival.
- mechanism: The opening cover overwhelms lowered, exhausted faces; the second cohort is softened and satisfied with directed striving immediately before the focus. The same broad geometry is reversed: being covered becomes protective interiority, and being high becomes release from abasement and fruitless strain.
- trace:
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B001: Concealment supplies the shared covering geometry whose value can be reversed.
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B008: Protective cover converts enclosure from an overwhelming exterior into a sheltering interior.
  - 88:10 **عَالِيَةٍ** ع ل و B001: Rising supplies the upward reversal of the earlier lowered state.
  - 88:1 **ٱلْغَٰشِيَةِ** غ ش و B002: An all-enveloping cover supplies the oppressive outer counterpart to the focus enclosure.
  - 88:2 **خَٰشِعَةٌ** خ ش ع B001: Lowering and submission establish the vertical condition that focus highness reverses.
  - 88:3 **نَّاصِبَةٌ** ن ص ب B004: Debilitating fatigue supplies the spent condition from which the second scene rises.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B002: Softness and ease mark the bodily texture of the protected destination.
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B001: Directed movement toward a sought end makes the garden the attained terminus rather than static scenery.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B001: Satisfaction supplies the evaluative completion of the upward transition.

## acoustic cognitive shelter
- reading: A high-order refuge for perception and understanding, sheltered from confused noise and misdirection.
- mechanism: The following negated hearing clause makes the enclosure a selective boundary: confused sound and deviation do not penetrate it, while understanding remains intact. A potentially negative mind-covering image is therefore reversed into protection of attention.
- trace:
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B006: Mind-veiling supplies the cognitive danger that the contextually purified enclosure excludes.
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B008: Protective covering turns the garden boundary into a guard for attention.
  - 88:10 **عَالِيَةٍ** ع ل و B002: High rank supplies qualitative order to the protected perceptual environment.
  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B003: Hearing as understanding makes the excluded disturbance cognitive as well as acoustic.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B003: Mixed noise supplies what the enclosure keeps from becoming its soundscape.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B005: Deviation from what is sound supplies the excluded directional disorder.

## inhabitable vertical order
- reading: A carefully graded habitat where elevation, access, flow, rest, alignment, and broad provision form one spatial system.
- mechanism: Flow, raised resting places, deliberately lowered or fixed vessels, aligned cushions, and dispersed carpets articulate both vertical and planar axes. Highness is not inaccessible remoteness; it is an inhabitable, serviced spatial order.
- trace:
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B003: The orchard enclosure supplies the bounded interior in which the later arrangement operates.
  - 88:10 **عَالِيَةٍ** ع ل و B001: Elevation supplies the master vertical axis subsequently repeated inside the scene.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B001: Continuous movement and spreading of water make the elevated enclosure materially live.
  - 88:13 **سُرُرٌ** س ر ر B011: A place of settled reclining makes height usable for repose.
  - 88:13 **مَّرْفُوعَةٌ** ر ف ع B001: Deliberate raising repeats the focus adjective at the scale of furnishings.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B001: Placement at a lower or appointed level counterbalances raising and makes provision reachable.
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: Linear alignment supplies horizontal order across the elevated interior.
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B001: Distribution supplies broad coverage rather than a single privileged point.

## created scale model
- reading: A miniature inhabited cosmos whose canopy, living elevations, solid relief, and spread fertile base reproduce the surah's larger created order.
- mechanism: The later invitation to inspect measured creation moves through an overhead canopy, raised expanse, solid elevations, fertile ground, and useful spread surface. Read back onto the focus, the garden becomes a compressed created world whose highness is engineered across several scales.
- trace:
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B011: Dense rising vegetation supplies the garden's living middle scale.
  - 88:10 **عَالِيَةٍ** ع ل و B005: The upper side supplies an orienting top to the garden's internal world.
  - 88:17 **يَنظُرُونَ** ن ظ ر B001: Directed sight and insight authorize close attention to construction rather than passive scenery.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B001: Measure and proportion supply a designed relation among the scene's scales.
  - 88:18 **ٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B004: What rises overhead and shades supplies a macro-canopy analogous to the garden's covering.
  - 88:18 **رُفِعَتْ** ر ف ع B001: Raising makes elevation an enacted construction rather than a static label.
  - 88:19 **ٱلْجِبَالِ** ج ب ل B001: A solid elevated mass supplies the supporting relief between upper canopy and ground.
  - 88:20 **ٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B002: Soft productive ground supplies the fertile base required by the garden.
  - 88:20 **سُطِحَتْ** س ط ح B005: A useful spread surface makes the created base inhabitable rather than merely flat.

## domination sense weakened
- reading: The abode's elevation is better carried as honor and shelter without domination; coercive superiority remains visible but weakened.
- mechanism: The focus adjective can activate superiority and mastery, but the later command is restricted to reminding while controlling oversight is explicitly denied. Context therefore weakens coercive highness and strengthens dignified, non-dominating elevation.
- trace:
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B008: Protective covering supplies care without requiring control over those sheltered.
  - 88:10 **عَالِيَةٍ** ع ل و B003: Haughty superiority supplies a live but contextually disfavored reading of highness.
  - 88:10 **عَالِيَةٍ** ع ل و B004: Overcoming mastery supplies the coercive mechanism that the later negation checks.
  - 88:10 **عَالِيَةٍ** ع ل و B002: Noble rank remains as the non-coercive qualitative alternative.
  - 88:21 **فَذَكِّرْ مُذَكِّرٌ** ذ ك ر B009: Reminder supplies an invitational mode of address bounded short of compulsion.
  - 88:22 **بِمُصَيْطِرٍ** س ط ر B003: Controlling supervision supplies precisely the role denied in the clause.

## two coverings and return
- reading: The protective destination at the end of right orientation, contrasted with a self-made covering that hides truth and must still return for reckoning.
- mechanism: The closing motion turns away, covers truth, returns to a destination, and enters reckoning. This activates two opposed uses of covering: the garden conceals in order to shelter, whereas refusal conceals what should remain visible; return and accounting assign the resulting orientation its destination and rank.
- trace:
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B001: Concealment supplies the common image whose function differs between refuge and refusal.
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B008: Protective covering marks the beneficial pole of concealment.
  - 88:10 **عَالِيَةٍ** ع ل و B002: High rank supplies the evaluated standing of the protective destination.
  - 88:23 **تَوَلَّىٰ** و ل ي B007: Turning away supplies the outward orientation opposite arrival within the shelter.
  - 88:23 **وَكَفَرَ** ك ف ر B003: Concealing truth supplies a harmful counterpart to the garden's protective concealment.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return to a destination turns the focus place into a possible terminus of orientation.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B001: Accounting supplies the sorting operation by which orientation becomes an assigned outcome.

## burial reversal
- reading: A reversal in which what was shrouded below returns and is raised into a high enclosure.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B009: Burial and shrouding supply the concealed-below starting pole.
  - 88:10 **عَالِيَةٍ** ع ل و B001: Rising supplies the opposite upper pole directly inside the focus phrase.
  - 88:18 **رُفِعَتْ** ر ف ع B001: Enacted raising reinforces movement from hidden low position to manifest height.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return to a destination supplies the trajectory completing the reversal.

## gestational renewal
- reading: Entry into a protected matrix of renewed life, as though the exhausted condition is released into a fresh and recovered one.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B007: Hidden gestation supplies protected life developing inside an enclosure.
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B014: Fresh beginning turns the enclosure into the start of a renewed condition.
  - 88:10 **عَالِيَةٍ** ع ل و B011: Recovery-specific rising supplies release from an afflicted state.
  - 88:1 **حَدِيثُ** ح د ث B001: Coming into being after absence supplies emergence into a new mode.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B002: The unloading-at-birth branch turns placement into release from enclosure and activates the gestational analogy.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B002: Creative origination supplies transition into a newly made condition.

## collective elevation
- reading: An elevated social body: sheltered persons gathered, ordered, and broadly accommodated as part of the garden's own image.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B013: The gathered human mass supplies a possible collective body inside the place noun.
  - 88:10 **عَالِيَةٍ** ع ل و B002: Noble standing supplies collective elevation rather than topographic height.
  - 88:8 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B004: Faces standing for persons turn the positive scene into an assembly of whole selves.
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: Alignment supplies coherent collective ordering rather than an undifferentiated mass.
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B001: Distribution supplies extension of that ordered collective across the enclosure.


# Focus 88:11

## selective soundscape
- reading: The place has a selective soundscape: hearing remains possible, while foul speech and confusing clamor do not enter it.
- mechanism: Ordinary hearing is available, but vain or foul speech and mixed clamor are absent; the pleasing-audition branch keeps open the possibility of positive sound.
- trace:
  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B001: Ear-hearing supplies the literal sensory channel that remains operative under the object-level negation.
  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B007: Pleasing audition supplies a positive acoustic alternative, so the line need not erase sound as such.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B002: Vain, false, or foul speech identifies the morally damaged content excluded from hearing.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B003: Mixed raised noise identifies acoustic confusion, making the exclusion environmental as well as semantic.

## no null uptake
- reading: Nothing heard there asks for understanding or compliance while being null, non-counting, or off-course.
- mechanism: The line excludes a communicative object that solicits cognitive uptake or obedience while being void, non-counting, or misdirected.
- trace:
  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B003: Understanding and compliance turn hearing from bare sensation into accepted or enacted communication.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B001: The thing discarded from reckoning supplies the null status of the communication being refused uptake.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B005: Deviation from correctness supplies the direction in which such apparently actionable speech would mislead.

## no empty public circulation
- reading: The community has no circulation economy in which empty report or reputation-performance becomes habitual speech.
- mechanism: The negation can govern a social circulation system: no empty report, reputation-performance, or habitual false discourse becomes common currency.
- trace:
  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B005: Public reputation and circulated report expand hearing from an individual ear to a social transmission network.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B001: What is set aside as not counting gives the circulated material its lack of worth.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B004: A group's repeated speech-habit supplies the mechanism by which empty talk could otherwise become a communal norm.

## no abusive transmission
- reading: The social relation itself is non-injurious: no speaker makes another person receive abuse, falsehood, or humiliation.
- mechanism: The line can describe a relation between agents, not merely a quiet recipient: no one injects verbal injury into another's hearing.
- trace:
  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B004: Causing another to hear supplies an upstream speaker-to-recipient transmission relation.
  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B006: Making someone hear abuse specifies the injurious use of that transmission relation.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B002: Foul speech, insult, and sinful talk supply the harmful payload that never reaches a hearer.

## arriving report filters public circulation
- reading: Communication is retained but sorted: consequential report can arrive and encompass, while report without weight never becomes the garden's public currency.
- mechanism: The surah opens with a report that arrives, renews itself as news, and concerns an encompassing event; this revises the focus from speechlessness to a contrast between consequential report and empty circulation.
- trace:
  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B005: Public circulation supplies the channel in which both the opening report and excluded empty report can travel.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B001: The non-counting thing supplies the criterion by which one kind of circulated material is screened out.
  - 88:1 **أَتَىٰكَ** ء ت ي B001: Arrival gives the opening report a directed path to a recipient, contrasting with excluded circulation.
  - 88:1 **حَدِيثُ** ح د ث B003: Renewed news supplies a form of speech that is not rejected merely because it is heard.
  - 88:1 **حَدِيثُ** ح د ث B004: Becoming the talk of people makes the public-circulation mechanism explicit rather than merely private.
  - 88:1 **ٱلْغَٰشِيَةِ** غ ش و B002: An encompassing cover supplies the report with consequential scope, opposing the weightlessness of laghw.

## failed intake becomes auditory diet
- reading: Laghw is communicative intake that can be forced into a hearer yet neither nourishes nor satisfies; the garden excludes that failed auditory diet.
- mechanism: The punitive scene repeatedly presents intake that enters but does not fulfill its function. That pattern recasts laghw as failed auditory nourishment and abusive speech as something poured into the inner person.
- trace:
  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B006: Making someone hear abuse supplies the painful communicative intake whose absence is being modeled.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B006: Failure supplies the shared functional defect: the speech enters a channel yet produces no good end.
  - 88:5 **تُسْقَىٰ** س ق ي B010: Watering the heart with something unwelcome directly bridges forced drink and injurious verbal reception.
  - 88:6 **طَعَامٌ** ط ع م B001: Tasting and taking food supplies the bodily intake frame transferred cautiously to hearing.
  - 88:7 **يُسْمِنُ** س م ن B001: Fatness opposed to emaciation supplies one expected function that the punitive food fails to perform.
  - 88:7 **يُغْنِى** غ ن ي B002: Sufficiency supplies the other failed function and defines what worthwhile intake should achieve.
  - 88:7 **جُوعٍ** ج و ع B001: The empty stomach supplies the unresolved need left behind by functionless intake.

## effort without verbal subjection
- reading: No heard directive subordinates agency to work that does not count; striving remains purposeful enough for its agent to own with satisfaction.
- mechanism: Exhausting work in one scene is answered by satisfaction with one's striving in the other. Hearing-as-compliance therefore becomes a question of agency: no null directive converts obedience into futile, alienated labor.
- trace:
