  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B003: Understanding followed by compliance supplies the possible command-to-action link.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B001: What does not count marks a directive or its resulting labor as purposeless rather than merely difficult.
  - 88:3 **عَامِلَةٌ** ع م ل B001: Intentional work supplies the action that may be either owned and fruitful or subordinated and futile.
  - 88:3 **نَّاصِبَةٌ** ن ص ب B004: Exhausting hardship supplies the bodily cost of the negative labor regime.
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B002: Work and earning supply the positive scene's retained agency rather than an absence of effort.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B001: Satisfaction supplies the changed relation between the agent and the result of striving.

## arranged acoustic environment
- reading: The line furnishes the setting acoustically: flowing and social sounds may remain, while unplaced, mixed clamor is the one element not distributed through the space.
- mechanism: The positive scene is organized by controlled motion and placement: water runs, couches are raised, cups placed, cushions aligned, and carpets spread. The focus line joins that inventory as acoustic ordering, not sensory vacancy.
- trace:
  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B001: Literal hearing makes sound another environmental channel alongside water, furniture, vessels, and textiles.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B003: Mixed clamor supplies the acoustic disorder excluded from the otherwise arranged scene.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B001: Flowing motion supplies continuous positive sound and movement, preventing the scene from collapsing into silence.
  - 88:13 **مَّرْفُوعَةٌ** ر ف ع B001: Elevation supplies one deliberate placement operation in the environmental sequence.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B001: Putting an object in a settled place supplies a second deliberate ordering operation.
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: Alignment on an even line turns amenity into visible arrangement rather than random abundance.
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B001: Controlled spreading supplies distribution across the space, against which uncontrolled sound-scatter is excluded.

## inspection displaces hearsay
- reading: No evidence-free signal is converted into suspicion or deviation; attention is redirected from empty hearsay toward forms whose manner can be inspected.
- mechanism: A rare hearing branch describes listening or looking, finding nothing, then acting on suspicion. The later command to direct sight toward measured creation reverses that mechanism: empty auditory inference gives way to inspectable form.
- trace:
  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B013: Suspicious hearing and looking supplies the defective epistemic sequence of weak perception followed by unsupported inference.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B005: Deviation from correctness supplies the result of acting on an empty perceptual cue.
  - 88:17 **يَنظُرُونَ** ن ظ ر B001: Directed sight or insight supplies the corrective act of attending to something available for inspection.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B001: Measurement and proportion supply structured evidence in the created object rather than an empty report.
  - 88:20 **سُطِحَتْ** س ط ح B001: An extended visible surface supplies the final inspectable plane in the repeated observation sequence.

## reminder without control
- reading: Speech can remind and restore awareness, but it never turns hearing into compelled submission, surveillance, or humiliation.
- mechanism: Speech remains as reminder and verbal recollection, but the speaker is explicitly not a controlling overseer. The focus therefore excludes communicative domination, not communication.
- trace:
  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B004: Making another hear supplies the communicative act whose social force is limited by the later non-control clause.
  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B006: Making another hear abuse supplies the coercive or humiliating transmission specifically absent from the focus environment.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B002: Foul and sinful speech supplies the verbal payload that would turn address into injury or domination.
  - 88:21 **فَذَكِّرْ مُذَكِّرٌ** ذ ك ر B003: Restoring something to awareness supplies a legitimate non-coercive purpose for speech.
  - 88:21 **فَذَكِّرْ مُذَكِّرٌ** ذ ك ر B004: Mention on the tongue confirms that the context retains verbal address rather than idealizing muteness.
  - 88:22 **لَّسْتَ** ل ي س B001: Negation of the present relation supplies the explicit limit placed on the addresser's authority.
  - 88:22 **بِمُصَيْطِرٍ** س ط ر B003: The controlling overseer supplies the domination model that reminder is denied permission to become.

## post accounting no null residue
- reading: After return and account, no void utterance, unsettled claim, or socially circulated nullity remains in the communicative field.
- mechanism: The not-counting branch of laghw becomes quasi-legal in a sequence that includes a right-document, written lines, return, and accounting. The garden is read as a post-accounting field from which null or unsettled speech leaves no residue.
- trace:
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B001: Speech or action discarded from count supplies the legal-accounting hinge of the revised reading.
  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B005: Public report supplies material whose social circulation can eventually be recorded, weighed, or excluded.
  - 88:21 **فَذَكِّرْ مُذَكِّرٌ** ذ ك ر B008: A document establishing a right supplies a juridical register for consequential words.
  - 88:22 **بِمُصَيْطِرٍ** س ط ر B001: An aligned written line supplies inscription and ordering without importing the overseer's coercive branch.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return to a destination supplies the temporal movement into final settlement.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B001: Counting and accounting activate the focus branch's explicit image of what is or is not reckoned.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B006: Reviewing a matter supplies deliberate adjudication rather than mechanical enumeration alone.

## cup instrument selective audition
- reading: The cup cluster faintly activates enjoyable audition: sonic pleasure may remain, while confused or intrusive sound alone is excluded.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B007: Pleasing audition supplies a positive sonic category that can coexist with the focus negation.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B003: Mixed clamor narrows what is absent and prevents the activation from becoming a claim of total silence.
  - 88:14 **وَأَكْوَابٌ** ك و ب B002: The homonymous entertainment-instrument branch weakly activates an auditory register inside the cup sequence.
  - 88:13 **سُرُرٌ** س ر ر B010: Hidden joy and ease supply the affective condition under which pleasing sound, rather than clamor, would belong.

## bucket handle balanced service
- reading: As a contained material analogy, the scene carries no useless load: water service is balanced, vessels are ready, and nothing non-counting strains the system.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B008: The bucket loop or balancing handle supplies the surprising material image of a burden carried without imbalance.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B001: The discarded non-counting thing supplies the useless load or component absent from the analogy.
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B006: A spring as the source of running water activates the bucket handle's water-service domain.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B001: Continuous flow supplies a service system in which burdens and vessels no longer need strained transport.
  - 88:14 **وَأَكْوَابٌ** ك و ب B001: The handleless cup supplies the receiving vessel and sharpens the contrast with the focus root's handled bucket image.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B001: Settled placement supplies readiness without needless carrying, balancing, or discarded effort.

## hearing shackle non domination
- reading: No utterance fastens the hearer into a verbal shackle; reminder can address without controlling.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B009: The shackle supplies a material image for speech that binds a hearer into compelled response.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B002: Foul or sinful speech supplies the verbal substance that could make listening function like restraint.
  - 88:21 **فَذَكِّرْ مُذَكِّرٌ** ذ ك ر B003: Restoring awareness supplies a communicative purpose that need not bind or overpower its recipient.
  - 88:22 **بِمُصَيْطِرٍ** س ط ر B003: Controlling oversight supplies the explicitly denied social mechanism that would turn hearing into a shackle.

## heard but not reached closure
- reading: Worthless report is also absent as unfinished circulation: nothing merely heard-of remains suspended without arrival, return, or settlement.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B011: Hearing without reaching supplies a liminal report that circulates without arriving at its proper endpoint.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B001: The non-counting thing identifies the kind of report denied an unresolved circulation state.
  - 88:1 **أَتَىٰكَ** ء ت ي B001: Arrival supplies the opening endpoint question for a report in motion.
  - 88:1 **حَدِيثُ** ح د ث B003: Renewed news supplies the report-object whose successful arrival opens the sequence.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return to a destination supplies the closing endpoint and removes indefinite, ownerless circulation.


# Focus 88:12

## hydraulic issuance
- reading: There is an issuing source whose identity is continuous outward flow, not a static pool.
- mechanism: The water-source branch identifies an issuing origin, while the flow branch makes transit rather than storage its defining condition.
- trace:
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B006: The springing water-source supplies the physical origin from which the scene is replenished.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B001: Running and flowing makes continuous outward motion the source's operative state.

## continuing provision
- reading: The verse can present the choicest good as an allowance kept in circulation: provision defined by continuance.
- mechanism: The choice-part sense of the noun and the continuing-grant sense of the adjective allow the current to function as maintained provision: the good is not merely possessed but kept available.
- trace:
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B014: The choice or finest part makes the source a selected excellence rather than a generic object.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B006: Continuing provision or grant turns motion into maintained availability.

## mobile watchfulness
- reading: A secondary reading lets the flowing source act as mobile watchfulness, making the place attentive as well as fertile.
- mechanism: A visual double exposure lies beneath the hydrological reading: the source is also an active eye, so the place appears watched over rather than merely watered.
- trace:
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B003: The eye of watchful care gives the noun a protective, attentive presence.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B001: Active running lets that watchfulness move through the scene instead of remaining a fixed gaze.

## arriving channel
- reading: The spring is also an event of access: a route comes into operation and keeps issuing within an encompassing reality.
- mechanism: The opening's arrival, newly occurring event, and encompassing cover recast the focus current as an emergence: a channel comes into operation inside a scene otherwise characterized by what overtakes and covers.
- trace:
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B006: The focus spring supplies the origin that can emerge within the encompassing scene.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B001: The focus flow supplies the motion that the arriving channel releases.
  - 88:1 **أَتَىٰكَ** ء ت ي B004: The watercourse and opened path image turns arrival into the activation of a route for flow.
  - 88:1 **حَدِيثُ** ح د ث B001: Coming to be after not being supplies the event-like emergence of the current.
  - 88:1 **ٱلْغَٰشِيَةِ** غ ش و B002: The all-enveloping cover supplies the field through which the localized source becomes a counter-opening.

## contrastive drinking modes
- reading: This is a counter-source whose freedom consists in running openly rather than being administered from a source fixed at terminal intensity.
- mechanism: The earlier source belongs to passive administered drinking and a reached terminal condition; the focus repeats the source noun but replaces that relation with self-sustaining motion. The contrast is therefore agency and temporal condition, not merely two temperatures.
- trace:
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B006: The repeated spring sense holds the object constant across the two scenes.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B001: Self-running flow gives the focus source its contrasting open mobility.
  - 88:5 **تُسْقَىٰ** س ق ي B001: Giving drink supplies an externally administered relation between drinker and source.
  - 88:5 **عَيْنٍ** ع ي ن B006: The identical spring branch makes the differing modifier and syntax carry the contrast.
  - 88:5 **ءَانِيَةٍ** ء ن ي B003: Reaching the appointed limit supplies a terminal, intensified condition opposed to ongoing transit.

## efficacious sufficiency
- reading: The source embodies provision that actually reaches and sustains its recipient, reversing intake that leaves need untouched.
- mechanism: The failed food is defined by its inability to sustain or fill, followed by a state of good condition. Against that sequence, the focus branch of continuing grant makes the current an efficacious supply rather than decorative abundance.
- trace:
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B006: The water-source gives the provision a concrete replenishing origin.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B006: Ongoing provision makes the flow durative and effective rather than ornamental.
  - 88:6 **طَعَامٌ** ط ع م B004: Provision, livelihood, and good condition establish the function that the denied food fails to perform.
  - 88:7 **يُغْنِى** غ ن ي B002: Sufficiency identifies the missing effect against which the continuing source is measured.
  - 88:7 **جُوعٍ** ج و ع B001: The empty belly supplies the unmet need that distinguishes real provision from mere intake.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B001: Good condition supplies the positive bodily and affective result of efficacious provision.

## motion answering effort
- reading: The spring is a reward still in motion, a current that keeps pace with and answers purposive striving.
- mechanism: Purposeful movement followed by acceptance activates the keeping-pace branch of the focus adjective. The current can be read as provision that continues alongside completed effort, not a motionless wage deposited afterward.
- trace:
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B014: The choice good supplies what responsive motion continually makes available.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B007: Keeping pace gives the current a responsive relation to another directed movement.
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B001: Purposeful movement toward a sought end supplies the motion with which the current keeps pace.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B001: The non-dominant mapped acceptance branch supplies the settled affective completion of that movement.

## clean acoustic current
- reading: The flowing source may be the ordered sound left audible when futile human noise has been removed.
- mechanism: The immediately preceding exclusion of mixed, worthless sound opens an acoustic slot. The moving water can fill it as ordered nonverbal sound, so silence here is selective rather than absolute.
- trace:
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B006: The water-source supplies a plausible positive sound-maker inside the otherwise purified soundscape.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B001: Continuous flowing supplies the patterned acoustic current.
  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B001: Auditory perception makes the preceding negation define what may enter the scene's hearing.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B003: Mixed noisy sound identifies what is absent, allowing ordered flow to become the audible remainder.

## distributed hospitality
- reading: The spring is the active supply within a deliberately distributed hospitality environment, linking abundance to ready access.
- mechanism: Cups ready in assigned places, objects aligned, and coverings distributed convert the lone spring into the live supply of an arranged reception system. Flow connects source to use across a prepared field.
- trace:
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B006: The spring supplies the central replenishing source for the prepared setting.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B001: Flow supplies the distribution link between source and multiple points of use.
  - 88:14 **وَأَكْوَابٌ** ك و ب B001: Handleless cups supply open receiving vessels suited to immediate communal use.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B001: Placement in an assigned lower position makes the vessels ready endpoints rather than stored objects.
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: Alignment on an even line supplies deliberate order across the receiving space.
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B001: Scattering and distribution extend availability throughout the setting.

## looked at landscape eye
- reading: The spring is also a landscape eye that makes abundance manifest and tutors the observer in how to look.
- mechanism: The command to direct sight or insight reactivates the focus noun's eye senses. The spring becomes a landscape eye: visibly issuing water and simultaneously training the observer's own seeing.
- trace:
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B002: Face-to-face seeing gives the spring noun an epistemic layer of manifest encounter.
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B006: The spring sense keeps the visual activation grounded in the actual water-source.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B001: Visible motion makes the landscape eye an event to inspect rather than a hidden abstraction.
  - 88:17 **يَنظُرُونَ** ن ظ ر B001: Directing sight or insight activates both optical and reflective attention toward created forms.

## designed gradient system
- reading: The spring is the visible outlet of a measured landscape system whose heights, barriers, and surfaces make circulation possible.
- mechanism: A measured water-holding recess, elevation, upright boundaries, and an extended surface provide the geometry that makes flow intelligible. The source is no longer isolated scenery but a circulation point embedded in designed terrain.
- trace:
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B006: The source supplies the water-entry point within the larger terrain system.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B001: Running water translates the surrounding vertical and horizontal forms into a working gradient.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B011: A shaped recess or well that retains water supplies the local holding form from which flow can issue.
  - 88:18 **رُفِعَتْ** ر ف ع B001: Raising supplies vertical difference and potential head.
  - 88:19 **نُصِبَتْ** ن ص ب B001: Erect prominent forms supply stable boundaries and channels in the terrain.
  - 88:20 **سُطِحَتْ** س ط ح B001: An even extended surface supplies the receiving plane across which water can spread.

## noncoercive circulation
- reading: The spring can also model guidance: a benefit sent into circulation and offered continuously, while forced reception is withheld.
- mechanism: Reminder that runs on the tongue and the explicit denial of controlling surveillance activate the envoy and habitual-course branches of flow. The source offers a model of guidance that circulates and remains accessible without forcing reception.
- trace:
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B006: The open source supplies an available origin from which a non-coercive benefit may be taken.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B003: The running agent or envoy lets the current model a message moving on an affair.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B002: A continuing customary course gives circulation regularity without compulsion.
  - 88:21 **فَذَكِّرْ مُذَكِّرٌ** ذ ك ر B004: Remembrance running on the tongue supplies the communicative current.
  - 88:22 **لَّسْتَ** ل ي س B001: Categorical negation removes domination from the messenger's assigned function.
  - 88:22 **بِمُصَيْطِرٍ** س ط ر B003: The controlling overseer supplies the coercive role explicitly excluded from circulation.

## uncovered evidence
- reading: The flowing source is manifest evidence: openness remains available even when a perceiver turns away and covers what was shown.
- mechanism: Turning away and covering truth reverse the focus's direct-seeing and visible-source branches. The current becomes open evidence whose availability does not guarantee that a viewer will face or acknowledge it.
- trace:
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B002: Direct face-to-face seeing makes the source an encounter with something manifest.
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B006: The visible flowing water-source grounds the epistemic reading in a concrete phenomenon.
  - 88:23 **تَوَلَّىٰ** و ل ي B007: Turning the back supplies the viewer's directional refusal of what is available to sight.
  - 88:23 **وَكَفَرَ** ك ف ر B003: Covering truth supplies the cognitive occlusion opposed to the source's manifestness.

## sweetness without withholding
- reading: The spring is an unwithheld current of sweetness, opposed not only to painful water but to the act of cutting benefit off.
- mechanism: One later root inventory holds sweet water, bodily abstention, withholding, and punitive pain together. That polarity activates the focus current as unwithheld sweetness: a supply whose beneficence lies in remaining open and moving.
- trace:
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B006: The focus source gives the sweetness activation a concrete water body.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B006: Ongoing grant makes the beneficial supply persist rather than close.
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B001: Sweet pleasant water supplies the positive pole latent beside the punitive surface use.
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B003: Withholding and weaning supply the closure that uninterrupted provision reverses.
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B005: Punitive pain supplies the explicit negative pole against which open sweetness becomes consequential.

## returning accounted flow
- reading: The current suggests ordered abundance: a regular circulation whose openness is compatible with return, destination, and measure.
- mechanism: Return to a homeward point and subsequent reckoning place the apparently open-ended current inside a larger circuit. Flow remains generous, but it is ordered movement with return and measure rather than directionless excess.
- trace:
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B006: The spring is the identifiable origin to which a circuit of movement can remain related.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B002: A regular course supplies ordered continuity rather than unbounded motion.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return to a destination bends the forward current into a homeward circuit.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B001: Counting and accounting supply measure and terminal intelligibility to the course.

## pressure aperture
- reading: The source can be modeled as a landscape aperture: a small designed opening where retained abundance becomes visible current.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B007: The thin eye-like opening in a skin supplies a pressure outlet from which water seeps.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B001: Continuous flow supplies what exits through the opening.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B011: The shaped notch or well that holds water supplies a designed reservoir behind the outlet.
  - 88:18 **ٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B001: The non-dominant mapped narrow aperture supplies a cross-domain opening through which something enters or emerges.

## human double exposure
- reading: For an instant the phrase also silhouettes a wide-eyed young attendant, adding animate hospitality without displacing the spring.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B016: Wide beautiful eyes supply the human visual feature behind the double exposure.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B004: The young woman or female servant supplies a possible human figure beneath the flowing adjective.

## liquid present value
- reading: The source can figure value made immediately present and continuously disbursed, a liquid grant that remains within an eventual account.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B011: Ready present wealth supplies value that is on hand rather than deferred or absent.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B006: Continuing provision turns present value into a maintained stream rather than a one-time holding.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B001: Accounting supplies the terminal ledger against which an ongoing stream remains intelligible.


# Focus 88:13

## elevated settled support
- reading: There are stable supports deliberately lifted into an upper position, so their height belongs to the scene's spatial design.
- mechanism: The settled-support branch of س ر ر combines with physical upward displacement in ر ف ع: the objects are couches or throne-like supports whose elevation is materially part of their arrangement.
- trace:
  - 88:13 **سُرُرٌ** س ر ر B011: The settled-support image supplies couches as stable places of repose.
  - 88:13 **مَّرْفُوعَةٌ** ر ف ع B001: Physical upward displacement makes height a concrete property of those supports.

## honored repose
- reading: The couches confer honored, ease-filled station: their elevation is social and affective as well as spatial.
- mechanism: A couch is a place of settled ease; the joy and prosperity branch of س ر ر colors its use, while the rank branch of ر ف ع turns being raised into dignified station rather than geometry alone.
- trace:
  - 88:13 **سُرُرٌ** س ر ر B010: Joy and ease give the couch an affective function beyond furnishing.
  - 88:13 **سُرُرٌ** س ر ر B011: Settled support keeps the reading anchored in the actual couch noun.
  - 88:13 **مَّرْفُوعَةٌ** ر ف ع B002: High rank converts elevation into honor and elevated standing.

## presented hospitality
- reading: The couches are already presented for reception, with elevation carrying readiness and approach as well as height.
- mechanism: The couches remain places of support, but ر ف ع contributes presentation and approach: they read as prepared supports brought into availability for reception.
- trace:
  - 88:13 **سُرُرٌ** س ر ر B011: The couch branch supplies the concrete support offered to a recipient.
  - 88:13 **مَّرْفُوعَةٌ** ر ف ع B004: Bringing near or presenting turns the raised state into prepared availability.

## inward ease made public
- reading: The visible raised couches can be carried as the public form of an inward, previously concealed ease.
- mechanism: س ر ر holds inward concealment beside joy and ease; ر ف ع can announce or publicize. Their conjunction permits the raised couches to externalize an inward delight that had been hidden.
- trace:
  - 88:13 **سُرُرٌ** س ر ر B001: Hidden inwardness supplies the concealed interior pole of the reading.
  - 88:13 **سُرُرٌ** س ر ر B010: Joy and ease identify what the concealed interior can contain.
  - 88:13 **مَّرْفُوعَةٌ** ر ف ع B005: Announcing or making a report public supplies outward manifestation.

## arriving report discloses covered ease
- reading: The arriving report of an enveloping event makes the raised couches a disclosed form of ease that had remained inward and covered.
- mechanism: The opening brings an arriving, renewed report about something that covers comprehensively. That covering/disclosure tension activates the focus's own concealment and publication branches, making the raised couches a revealed exterior of hidden ease.
- trace:
  - 88:13 **سُرُرٌ** س ر ر B001: Inward concealment gives the focus scene something latent that can be disclosed.
  - 88:13 **مَّرْفُوعَةٌ** ر ف ع B005: Public announcement gives raising a revelatory direction.
  - 88:1 **أَتَىٰكَ** ء ت ي B001: Arrival makes disclosure an event that reaches the hearer.
  - 88:1 **حَدِيثُ** ح د ث B003: Renewed speech or report supplies the communicative vehicle of manifestation.
  - 88:1 **حَدِيثُ** ح د ث B006: Bringing something out and showing it sharpens the report into disclosure.
  - 88:1 **ٱلْغَٰشِيَةِ** غ ش و B001: A covering that rises over and conceals supplies the counterforce to disclosure.

## worked down to honored support
- reading: They enact a reversal: bodies and faces worn downward by labor are given support through which bearing, ease, and honored standing return.
- mechanism: The paired faces move from lowered posture, intentional labor, and exhausting strain to softness, earned outcome, and satisfaction. The elevated couch therefore becomes the bodily and social counter-form to being worked down: support that restores bearing and standing.
- trace:
  - 88:13 **سُرُرٌ** س ر ر B011: Settled support provides the bodily endpoint opposed to exhausting strain.
  - 88:13 **مَّرْفُوعَةٌ** ر ف ع B002: High rank turns restored posture into restored standing and honor.
  - 88:2 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B001: The face as exposed front makes the earlier condition bodily visible.
  - 88:2 **خَٰشِعَةٌ** خ ش ع B001: Lowering and submission establish the downward posture that elevation reverses.
  - 88:3 **عَامِلَةٌ** ع م ل B001: Intentional work supplies the sustained exertion behind the depleted posture.
  - 88:3 **نَّاصِبَةٌ** ن ص ب B004: Weariness and affliction make the labor physically consuming.
  - 88:8 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B006: Prestige and social face supply the restored outward standing.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B002: Softness and ease supply the felt quality of repose.
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B002: Work and earning connect the later condition to completed effort.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B001: Satisfaction marks the reversal as accepted fulfillment rather than mere cessation.

## deprivation to restorative reception
- reading: They are stations of bodily restoration, reversing an earlier system that takes in drink and food yet leaves the recipient heated, unsupported, and hungry.
- mechanism: The earlier scene routes drink and food through heat, failed sufficiency, and an empty belly. Against that failed reception, the focus's support, joy, and ease become embodied restoration rather than decorative luxury.

  - 88:13 **سُرُرٌ** س ر ر B010: Joy and ease supply the positive condition produced by restorative reception.

  - 88:7 **يُغْنِى** غ ن ي B002: Sufficiency is explicitly denied, defining deprivation as failed function.
  - 88:7 **جُوعٍ** ج و ع B001: The empty belly supplies the unresolved bodily need.

## secluded acoustic refuge
- reading: They are secluded elevated resting places inside a vegetation-screened and noise-excluding refuge, allowing privacy and honor to coexist.
- mechanism: A garden screened by vegetation, placed high, and defined by the non-hearing of mixed futile noise activates the inward/private branch of س ر ر. The couches become elevated refuges with visual and acoustic boundaries, not merely conspicuous seats.

  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B001: Auditory perception supplies the sensory boundary that the negation closes.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B003: Mixed noisy sound identifies what is excluded from the enclosure.

## arranged service topology
- reading: They are the elevated resting layer of an engineered hospitality topology that coordinates water flow, reachable vessels, ordered cushions, and floor-wide coverings.
- mechanism: Flowing water, handleless cups placed down, cushions aligned, and carpets dispersed organize motion, reach, and surface around the raised supports. The focus is one vertical component in a designed field of access: flow comes through, vessels are ready below, supports rise, rows gather, and floor coverings spread.

  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B006: The water source supplies an origin point within the furnished environment.

  - 88:14 **وَأَكْوَابٌ** ك و ب B001: Handleless cups supply immediately usable vessels beside the supports.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B001: Putting something in a lower or fixed place contrasts with and serves the raised layer.
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: Alignment on an even line supplies ordered lateral repetition.
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B001: Dispersal supplies broad coverage across the floor plane.

## human scale created architecture
- reading: Their elevation repeats the construction logic of the inspected world: a proportioned support lifted over a plane, a small architecture nested beneath the raised sky.

## presented not coercive seat
- reading: The support is presented as a destination of reception without making the human reminder an act of coercive enthronement.
- mechanism: The focus branch of presenting something to authority initially permits throne-like control, but the later command limits the human role to reminder and explicitly denies controlling oversight. The raised support can therefore read as a presented invitation or outcome rather than an instrument of compulsion.

# Focus 88:14

## ready drinking stations
- reading: Handleless drinking vessels have already been stationed where use can begin without retrieval or further preparation.
- mechanism: Handleless cups require direct bodily approach, while setting each object in a determined place removes fetching and preparation from the drinker's side. The line depicts readiness for use rather than a still-life inventory.
- trace:
  - 88:14 **وَأَكْوَابٌ** ك و ب B001: The handleless drinking-vessel image identifies an object meant for direct access and use.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B001: Setting something down in its determined place supplies the completed preparation that makes the cups ready.

## configured service array
- reading: The cups form a deliberately configured plurality in which placement assigns each vessel a role within an accessible array.
- mechanism: The attachment image in ك و ب makes the many units compositional, and the assigned-place image in و ض ع gives each unit a station. Together they support a service array rather than an accidental heap.
- trace:
  - 88:14 **وَأَكْوَابٌ** ك و ب B004: Attaching one part upon another makes the vessel plurality available as a coordinated configuration.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B006: Loads or persons assigned to a fixed place supply the functional image of each cup occupying a service station.

## entrusted hospitality
- reading: The cups are provision deliberately relinquished into the beneficiary's reach, making hospitality a transfer rather than a display.
- mechanism: The objects are not only prepared but handed over: placement transfers drinking capacity into another's reach or keeping. Hospitality is therefore rendered as relinquished control over a usable provision.
- trace:
  - 88:14 **وَأَكْوَابٌ** ك و ب B005: Drinking by means of the cup turns the displayed noun toward the act it enables.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B010: Depositing something with another recasts completed placement as a transfer of usable provision.

## administered versus offered drink
- reading: The cups reverse the earlier drink mechanism: coercive delivery from an extreme source gives way to portioned drink made available without compulsion.
- mechanism: The earlier drink scene couples being made to drink with a spring and a content that has reached an extreme state. Against that apparatus, the focus ayah relocates drinking into vessels already set down. The changed variable is agency: imposed intake from a source becomes unforced access to prepared portions.
- trace:
  - 88:14 **وَأَكْوَابٌ** ك و ب B005: Drinking from a cup anchors the revised model in the use latent in the focus object.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B001: Completed placement makes the favorable drink available before any action is demanded of its recipient.
  - 88:5 **تُسْقَىٰ** س ق ي B001: Giving someone drink supplies the contrasting causative relation in which intake is administered.
  - 88:5 **عَيْنٍ** ع ي ن B006: The flowing water source supplies an undivided origin from which the earlier recipients are made to drink.
  - 88:5 **ءَانِيَةٍ** ء ن ي B003: Reaching full maturity or limit sharpens the source's extreme condition against the neutral readiness of the placed cups.

## downward service in high estate
- reading: Within a high estate, the cups' downward placement performs service: status rises for the persons while useful objects are made low and reachable.
- mechanism: A low-rank or humbling branch of the focus placement word is activated between persons in good condition and a high garden. The apparent contradiction resolves functionally: persons are elevated while service objects descend into reach. Lowness becomes accommodation rather than degradation.
- trace:
  - 88:14 **وَأَكْوَابٌ** ك و ب B001: The handleless vessel makes bodily reach especially relevant to where the object is lowered or placed.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B005: Lowness of rank and humility supplies a social-vertical shadow to the cups' physical placement.
  - 88:8 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B004: The face standing for the whole person identifies the beneficiaries whose position is being contrasted with the objects.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B001: Good condition and beneficence supply the recipients' favorable estate.
  - 88:10 **عَالِيَةٍ** ع ل و B002: Elevation of rank lets the garden's height operate socially as well as spatially.

## hydraulic furnishing topology
- reading: The cups are the middle transfer layer of an engineered hospitality field, connecting continuous supply to elevated rest and distributing access across the furnished surface.
- mechanism: The sequence around 88:14 moves from continuous flow to resting places raised, cups stationed, cushions aligned, and carpets dispersed. The cups become transfer nodes between a resource and bodies at rest; their placement mediates vertical elevation and horizontal distribution.
- trace:
  - 88:14 **وَأَكْوَابٌ** ك و ب B005: Cup-mediated drinking gives the central objects a transfer function rather than a merely decorative one.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B006: Assignment to a determined station makes each cup a node in the surrounding arrangement.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B006: Continuing provision or supply gives the upstream flow a sustaining function that the cups can portion.
  - 88:13 **سُرُرٌ** س ر ر B011: A place of settled rest identifies the bodies and activity around which the service nodes are arranged.
  - 88:13 **مَّرْفُوعَةٌ** ر ف ع B001: Physical raising supplies the vertical coordinate immediately before the focus placement.
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: Alignment on an even line supplies ordered horizontal adjacency after the cups.
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B001: Dispersal supplies broad coverage, extending the local cup stations into a complete inhabited surface.

## domestic cosmography
- reading: Their exact placement miniaturizes the later world-order: hospitality and habitat are made intelligible by the same measured allocation of forms to positions.
- mechanism: The later inspection sequence repeats made, raised, erected, and spread forms at world scale. Its measured vertical and horizontal operations reactivate the focus participle: setting cups is the domestic-scale member of the same grammar of assigning forms to useful positions.
- trace:
  - 88:14 **وَأَكْوَابٌ** ك و ب B004: Parts attached into a configuration let the cup plurality behave as a small constructed order.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B001: Setting an object in a determined place anchors the domestic scene in the later sequence of world-shaping operations.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B001: Measuring and proportioning supplies deliberate calibration rather than unexplained existence.
  - 88:18 **رُفِعَتْ** ر ف ع B001: Raising establishes the cosmic vertical operation that echoes the raised furniture before the focus.
  - 88:19 **نُصِبَتْ** ن ص ب B001: Erecting something visibly upright supplies fixed placement at monumental scale.
  - 88:20 **سُطِحَتْ** س ط ح B001: An extended level surface supplies the horizontal field on which smaller useful arrangements can be placed.

## provision at the place of return
- reading: The cups are provision lodged at the destination in advance, making arrival a return to already sufficient hospitality.
- mechanism: Return to a place and reckoning-as-sufficiency pull the deposited-provision baseline toward destination logic. The cups read as an already credited sufficiency waiting at the terminus, not as a temporary amenity encountered en route.
- trace:
  - 88:14 **وَأَكْوَابٌ** ك و ب B005: The act of drinking identifies the concrete benefit made available at the destination.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B010: A deposit left with another supplies the image of provision already lodged ahead of its beneficiary.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return to a home or place supplies the terminal location toward which the prepared provision can be read.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B003: Sufficiency and enrichment activate the cups as an adequate provision rather than an ornamental quantity.

## silenced instrument shadow
- reading: At the level of lexical shadow, even an instrument latent in the cup-root has been laid down: abundance remains, but clamor is disarmed.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:14 **وَأَكْوَابٌ** ك و ب B002: The drum or reed-instrument image supplies a latent sonic object behind the ordinary cup surface.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B001: Setting the object down lets the activated instrument-shadow be physically stilled.
  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B007: Pleasurable hearing and music make the absent sonic channel specific enough to activate the instrument branch.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B003: Mixed noisy sound supplies what the scene excludes, turning set-down sonic objects into an image of tranquility.

## gameboard hospitality
- reading: As a contained spatial analogy, the cups become positioned units in a legible field whose rows, spread, and count imply designed relational order.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:14 **وَأَكْوَابٌ** ك و ب B003: The chessboard or game-object image activates a field of discrete pieces and positions behind the vessel plurality.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B006: Units assigned to fixed places make the cups behave visually like positioned pieces.
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: Straight alignment supplies one organizing rule for the surrounding field.
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B001: Distribution across space supplies the complementary rule of coverage rather than a single row.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B001: Counting supplies a remote terminal echo of discrete, positionable units.

## anthropomorphic cup stations
- reading: As a visual-material outlier, their necked silhouettes and assigned stations echo the resting occupants, making service objects into quiet proxies for a fully populated scene.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:14 **وَأَكْوَابٌ** ك و ب B006: A thin neck and enlarged head supply an anthropomorphic silhouette latent in the cup-root.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B006: Persons assigned to a fixed place let the repeated vessel silhouettes be imagined as stationed bodies.
  - 88:8 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B004: The face as a stand-in for the person supplies the nearby bodily scale for the visual analogy.
  - 88:13 **سُرُرٌ** س ر ر B011: A resting or reclining place situates bodies near the stationed cup silhouettes.


# Focus 88:15

## linear seating order
- reading: The cushions have been deliberately repeated into an even, readable seating order with a place-by-place rhythm.
- mechanism: Parallel repetition turns separate cushions into a legible seating line: each item remains distinct, but equal spacing makes the plurality operate as one prepared order.
- trace:
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: The straight-row image supplies the literal alignment and makes repeated cushions function as one ordered line.

## fitted rest interface
- reading: It describes rest as built into the environment through a continuous series of fitted supports.
- mechanism: The row is not only ornamental. It forms a continuous soft interface between bodies and a supporting structure, like an installed bench facing or a fitted pad.
- trace:
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B004: The bench-or-pad branch makes the aligned cushions an installed bodily interface rather than loose decoration.
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: The row branch extends the fitted surface serially across multiple resting positions.

## convergent gathering
- reading: The row is gathering infrastructure that coordinates plural access to a shared setting.
- mechanism: A row can coordinate convergence: it gives a plurality of arrivals distinct positions around a shared center without dissolving them into a crowd.
- trace:
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B007: The gathering-at-water branch turns alignment into infrastructure for shared access and assembly.
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: The even row preserves a distinct place for each participant within the convergence.

## serial abundance
- reading: Multiplicity becomes serial capacity: a common abundance has been converted into many ready receptacles for rest.
- mechanism: The branch activates a distributive image: one abundant provision is received through several parallel receptacles. By analogy, the cushion row multiplies a single condition of rest into many available places.
- trace:
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B002: The multiple-vessel camel image supplies abundance distributed across parallel receivers and a stabilizing aligned posture.

## toil transferred to furnishing
- reading: The row is a reversal of coerced exertion: objects hold formation so restored bodies no longer have to.
- mechanism: The earlier bodies are lowered, working, and exhausted. Against that sequence, the focus passive transfers disciplined alignment from persons to furnishings: the cushions now bear the row, leaving their recipients free to rest rather than stand in a laboring or martial rank.
- trace:
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: The branch ranges from ordinary rows to human and battle lines, allowing the focus to shift the burden of ranking onto cushions.
  - 88:2 **خَٰشِعَةٌ** خ ش ع B001: The lowered, submissive posture supplies the bodily state from which fitted rest is a reversal.
  - 88:3 **عَامِلَةٌ** ع م ل B001: Purposeful labor supplies the exertion that the prepared seating suspends.
  - 88:3 **نَّاصِبَةٌ** ن ص ب B004: Exhausting toil gives the contrast causal force: ordered supports answer bodily depletion.

## provision distributed without failure
- reading: The row becomes an operational hospitality network that turns a shared, sufficient provision into many accessible places.
- mechanism: An earlier service chain delivers drink and food yet fails to give sufficiency; the later flowing source reverses that failure. The focus branches for gathering at water and filling several vessels let the row function as a distribution topology, with many resting positions arranged around adequate provision.
- trace:
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B007: Gathering at water makes the cushion line organize access to a shared source.
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B002: The several-vessel image supplies a serial model for distributing one abundance among many receivers.
  - 88:5 **تُسْقَىٰ** س ق ي B001: Serving drink supplies the service relation whose harmful form is later reversed.
  - 88:6 **طَعَامٌ** ط ع م B004: Food as sustenance and good condition makes nourishment, not mere presentation, the relevant test.
  - 88:7 **يُغْنِى** غ ن ي B002: The sufficiency branch identifies what the earlier provisioning explicitly fails to achieve.
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B006: The flowing water source supplies the adequate center around which serial access can be organized.

## earned rest made spatial
- reading: The cushions are effort's completed spatial answer: satisfaction has become a prepared place where the body can stop striving.
- mechanism: Soft well-being is explicitly related to completed effort and satisfaction. The fitted row makes that relation spatial: prior striving has an outcome one can physically enter, occupy, and lean into.
- trace:
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B004: The fitted support branch gives bodily form to the transition from effort into rest.
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: Repeated alignment makes the rest publicly and plurally available rather than private or accidental.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B002: Softness and ease supply the bodily quality realized by the cushions.
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B002: Work and earned action supply the completed movement whose result is now inhabitable.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B001: Contentment closes the causal arc from effort to a fitting place of repose.

## quiet social choreography
- reading: The alignment is social and acoustic architecture, permitting communal presence without crowd-noise or rivalry.
- mechanism: A sheltered garden removes mixed, worthless noise. Within it, rows do social work: they gather people while preserving intervals and orientation, producing co-presence without the acoustic disorder of an undifferentiated crowd.
- trace:
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B007: The gathering branch supplies plural convergence rather than solitary comfort.
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: Even spacing converts convergence into calm social choreography.
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B003: The tree-sheltered garden supplies the bounded social environment in which the row operates.
  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B001: Literal hearing makes acoustic experience an explicit property of the setting.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B003: Mixed din supplies the excluded crowd-condition against which ordered gathering is heard.

## four operation furnishing field
- reading: It contributes the linear operator in a designed field: elevated support, fixed service, serial bodily access, and distributed floor space work together.
- mechanism: The adjacent furnishings enact four nonredundant operations: support is elevated, vessels are set at access points, cushions are serialized, and floor coverings are dispersed. The cushion row is the middle-scale connector between fixed stations and an open field.
- trace:
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: Linear serialization supplies the connector between isolated placements and wide dispersal.
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B004: The fitted bench-or-pad image makes that connector usable at bodily scale.
  - 88:13 **سُرُرٌ** س ر ر B011: A place of settling and leaning supplies the supported resting station.
  - 88:13 **مَّرْفُوعَةٌ** ر ف ع B001: Elevation establishes the vertical layer of the furnishing field.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B001: Placement in a determined position establishes discrete service points.
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B001: Dispersal supplies the broad floor field that prevents the row from becoming total regimentation.

## domestic microcosm of creation
- reading: Their measurable row is a small, inhabitable model of the same intelligible ordering later inspected across bodies, sky, mountains, and ground.
- mechanism: The later invitation to inspect measured creation scales up the furnishing grammar. What is raised, erected, leveled, and deliberately formed in the world echoes the elevated, placed, rowed, and spread interior; the cushion line becomes a domestic-scale instance of intelligible construction.
- trace:
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: The even line supplies a visibly inspectable act of local ordering.
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B005: The smooth level-ground branch lets the furnishing row connect to the later treatment of terrain.
  - 88:17 **يَنظُرُونَ** ن ظ ر B001: Directed perception turns arrangement from scenery into evidence to be examined.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B001: Measuring and proportioning supply the design principle shared by local rows and large structures.
  - 88:18 **رُفِعَتْ** ر ف ع B001: Cosmic elevation scales up the earlier vertical furnishing operation.
  - 88:19 **نُصِبَتْ** ن ص ب B001: Erecting a prominent solid supplies a fixed counterpart to the row's horizontal ordering.
  - 88:20 **سُطِحَتْ** س ط ح B001: The extended level surface supplies the world-scale field on which local alignment becomes legible.

## order without coercion
- reading: The row is hospitable order without human compulsion: places are prepared and intelligible, yet entry into them is not policed.
- mechanism: The packet juxtaposes a line-like root with the explicit denial of controlling oversight, then permits turning away. This distinguishes environmental order from imposed alignment: the cushions offer prepared places, but no human controller forces occupants into the row.
- trace:
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: The straight row supplies visible order with a latent martial or disciplinary edge.
  - 88:21 **فَذَكِّرْ مُذَكِّرٌ** ذ ك ر B003: Reminder as restored awareness supplies influence through recognition rather than compulsion.
  - 88:22 **بِمُصَيْطِرٍ** س ط ر B001: The written or aligned line creates a direct formal echo of the focus row.
  - 88:22 **بِمُصَيْطِرٍ** س ط ر B003: Controlling surveillance supplies the agency explicitly denied in the source construction.
  - 88:23 **تَوَلَّىٰ** و ل ي B007: Turning away keeps refusal structurally possible and therefore prevents the row from implying forced participation.

## readied destination for return
- reading: The row is anticipatory destination-space: exact, sufficient places stand ready for those who will return.
- mechanism: Return to an abode and subsequent reckoning turn empty-looking furnishings into terminal reception architecture. A row supplies enumerable, sufficient places awaiting arrivals, so the scene reads prospectively rather than as a still life.
- trace:
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: The repeated row makes future reception place-specific and countable.
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B004: The fitted support image makes the destination bodily occupiable rather than merely visible.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return to an abode supplies arriving occupants and a terminal direction for the prepared places.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B001: Counting and accounting make the serial places legible as exact rather than vaguely abundant.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B003: Sufficiency allows enumeration to imply provision adequate to each arrival rather than scarcity.

## camel vessel service line
- reading: They form a stable many-receiver service line, patterned by the camel branch's joined images of bodily alignment and distributed yield.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B002: The camel's stabilized posture and several filled vessels supply the outlier's bodily and distributive mechanism.
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B006: A flowing source supplies what can feed the serial service arrangement.
  - 88:14 **وَأَكْوَابٌ** ك و ب B001: Handleless drinking vessels give concrete receivers to the multiple-vessel activation.
  - 88:17 **ٱلْإِبِلِ** ء ب ل B001: The explicit camel and its care reactivate the otherwise remote camel branch of the focus root.

## reckoning as cushioned allotment
- reading: Each cushion becomes a material token of exact and sufficient allotment: reckoning is rendered as a place prepared for each.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: The straight row turns plural cushions into discrete positions that can be enumerated.
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B004: The fitted pad branch keeps the activation materially anchored in seating.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B008: The rare cushion-associated branch creates the direct cross-domain spark between final accounting and the focus object.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B001: Counting supplies the directional move from a row of objects to exact individual allotments.

## riparian soft boundary

# Focus 88:16

## areal distribution
- reading: Many carpets have been deliberately distributed into an accessible field that extends comfort across the surface.
- mechanism: The physical branch presents separation and spreading as a completed result. The carpets are not one floor covering but a multiplicity distributed across an area, making the surface broadly available.
- trace:
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B001: Physical scattering and laying out supply the focus's completed areal-distribution mechanism.

## disclosed interior
- reading: The carpets make an inward condition of welcome and ease visible throughout the environment.
- mechanism: A branch normally used for bringing concealed feeling or news outward lets the material arrangement function as exteriorized interiority. What would remain inward as welcome, ease, or generosity becomes publicly legible in the spread furnishings.
- trace:
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B002: Disclosure of concealed inward content turns the visible spread into a material showing of otherwise hidden welcome.

## inspectable layout
- reading: The eye encounters a layout opened out so that its order and contents can be examined.
- mechanism: The branch is form-distant because it is associated with an extended reduplicated use, but it keeps the core motion of opening a matter out. The laid carpets can therefore be carried as an inspectable layout, a scene whose contents have been exposed rather than merely ornamented.
- trace:
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B003: Investigating and exposing a matter supplies the exploratory sense of a scene opened out for inspection.

## covering reversed into disclosure
- reading: The carpets are a counter-covering: they occupy the surface while disclosing, rather than obscuring, the character of the place.
- mechanism: The opening joins a report made manifest to an event that envelops. Against that pairing, the focus becomes a reversal within the domain of covering: carpets cover a surface, yet their being spread makes welcome visible rather than suppressing perception.
- trace:
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B002: Outward disclosure anchors the focus as a covering that reveals an inward condition.
  - 88:1 **حَدِيثُ** ح د ث B006: Making something appear supplies the opening movement from hidden event to manifest report.
  - 88:1 **ٱلْغَٰشِيَةِ** غ ش و B002: An all-enveloping cover supplies the opposing motion against which the focus's disclosure becomes legible.

## repose distributed after striving
- reading: The spread carpets make restorative ease spatially plentiful, an embodied answer to effort rather than wealth alone.
- mechanism: Exhaustion in the first face-sequence is answered by softness and satisfied striving in the second. The focus's physical distribution then does more than multiply luxury: it spatially allocates repose as the realized yield of effort.
- trace:
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B001: Areal spreading makes repose available throughout the scene rather than at a single privileged point.
  - 88:3 **نَّاصِبَةٌ** ن ص ب B004: Weariness that depletes a person supplies the negative bodily state against which distributed rest changes value.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B002: Softness and ease supply the bodily quality that the spread carpets extend across space.
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B002: Work and earning supply the prior exertion for which the furnished field can be read as outcome.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B001: Satisfaction rather than displeasure supplies the affective completion of the effort-to-repose mechanism.

## silent material broadcast
- reading: Their visible spread is itself a silent announcement of welcome and ordered peace.
- mechanism: The scene excludes audible, mixed, futile speech immediately before presenting its material order. The focus's disclosure branch can therefore shift media: the carpets broadcast welcome and composure silently, through visible distribution rather than utterance.
- trace:
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B002: The branch's news-and-feeling disclosure supplies communication, while the carpets keep that communication material and nonverbal.
  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B001: Auditory perception supplies the channel explicitly denied within the scene.
  - 88:11 **لَٰغِيَةً** ل غ و B003: Mixed noisy sound specifies what is absent, allowing ordered surfaces to carry the communicative load.

## hidden delight exteriorized
- reading: The carpets complete the exteriorization of delight: inward ease has spread until it becomes the environment itself.
- mechanism: The couch-root holds together a place of reclining and concealed joy. When followed by the focus branch for disclosing inner content, the furnishing sequence becomes a conversion of inward delight into an inhabitable exterior.
- trace:
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B002: Disclosure of what is held inward supplies the outward phase of the affect-to-environment conversion.
  - 88:13 **سُرُرٌ** س ر ر B010: Concealed joy and ease supply the inward affect that the later spread renders visible.
  - 88:13 **سُرُرٌ** س ر ر B011: A place of settling and reclining keeps the affective reading attached to literal furniture.

## vertical point line field topology
- reading: The carpets' dispersion is the ordered completion of the room's topology, filling the plane left by elevated, positioned, and aligned objects.
- mechanism: The adjacent modifiers encode distinct spatial operations: couches are elevated, cups set at positions, cushions aligned, and carpets dispersed. The focus is the areal term in a progression from vertical level through points and lines to a field, so apparent scattering completes rather than breaks the order.
- trace:
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B001: Physical distribution supplies the final field-like spatial operation.
  - 88:13 **مَّرْفُوعَةٌ** ر ف ع B001: Raising supplies the vertical coordinate of the furnishing layout.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B001: Setting an object in a determined place supplies positional placement.
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: Alignment on a straight line supplies linear order immediately before areal spread.

## domestic surface to cosmic surface
- reading: The carpets are both interior furnishing and small-scale earth, a hinge that teaches the eye to inspect the world's laid-out surfaces.
- mechanism: After the carpeted surface, attention is redirected to how created things are formed, ending with the earth made level and usable. An earth branch that itself evokes a large carpet makes the scale bridge unusually explicit: the domestic spread becomes a miniature rehearsal for reading the terrestrial spread.
- trace:
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B001: Physical spreading supplies the shared surface operation at domestic scale.
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B003: Investigative exposure lets the furnished layout become a prompt to inspect how a surface has been made.
  - 88:17 **يَنظُرُونَ** ن ظ ر B001: Directed sight and insight convert the preceding visible layout into an object of reflective inspection.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B001: Proportioning and measuring supply the workmanship question that links arranged furnishings to created forms.
  - 88:20 **ٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B005: The earth-root's large spread-fabric image supplies the direct material bridge from carpet to terrain.
  - 88:20 **سُطِحَتْ** س ط ح B001: A level extended surface supplies the large-scale counterpart to the carpeted plane.

## noncoercive visual reminder
- reading: The carpets are quietly laid-out evidence: they invite recognition through inspection without functioning as instruments of control.
- mechanism: The later command is to reactivate remembrance while explicitly denying coercive oversight. The focus's exposure branch permits the carpets to join a noncoercive evidentiary mode: signs are laid out before perception rather than forced into assent.
- trace:
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B003: Opening a matter for examination anchors the carpets as a visible, inspectable presentation.
  - 88:21 **فَذَكِّرْ مُذَكِّرٌ** ذ ك ر B009: A reminder that reactivates memory supplies the intended cognitive effect of the displayed signs.
  - 88:22 **بِمُصَيْطِرٍ** س ط ر B003: Coercive supervisory control supplies the mode explicitly negated, distinguishing display from compulsion.

## blessing revealed against covering
- reading: Their spread is the act of making benefit undeniable and visible, the material inverse of covering a blessing.
- mechanism: The favorable face-sequence names ease and benefaction, while the later refusal-root includes covering a blessing. The focus's inward-disclosure branch places the carpets on the opposite side of that semantic action: benefaction is spread into visibility rather than covered over.
- trace:
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B002: Revealing concealed content makes the carpets an enacted manifestation rather than a neutral possession.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B001: Benefaction and good condition identify what the furnished scene makes manifest.
  - 88:23 **وَكَفَرَ** ك ف ر B004: Covering a blessing supplies the contrary response against which the carpets' visible disclosure gains moral force.

## account cushion loop
- reading: A contained branch echo lets the laid-out furnishings faintly prefigure a matter laid open for final review.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B003: Investigative exposure allows the furnishing layout to faintly anticipate matters being opened for review.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B006: Considering and inspecting a matter supplies the remote procedural link from laid-out scene to reckoning.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B008: The root's cushion branch unexpectedly loops the final accounting word back to the furnishing field.

## dispersion return count cycle
- reading: Their distributed plurality can also echo one phase in a larger rhythm: things spread outward, return, and are finally counted.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B001: Dispersion supplies the outward phase of the proposed distribute-return-count cycle.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return to a destination supplies the reversal of outward distribution.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B001: Counting and accounting supply the terminal gathering of distributed particulars into a reckoned whole.


# Focus 88:17

## proportioned living design
- reading: The ayah commissions close inspection of how a living form has been measured into a coordinated whole.
- mechanism: Sight is asked to become inspection: the camel's visible, complete form is evidence of prior measuring and proportioning, so createdness is encountered in relations among parts rather than asserted abstractly.
- trace:
  - 88:17 **يَنظُرُونَ** ن ظ ر B001: Directing sight or insight supplies the empirical act of examining the animal.
  - 88:17 **ٱلْإِبِلِ** ء ب ل B001: Camels and the skilled tending of their herds keep the inspection attached to a living, handled creature.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B001: Measuring and proportioning turns creation into an inspectable coordination of dimensions and functions.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B003: Complete, balanced visible form makes the result of that proportioning available to the eye.

## water independent fitness
- reading: Creation also means that the camel was made fit for continuity under water scarcity.
- mechanism: The interrogative about manner can be heard as a question of ecological fit: how was this creature made apt to continue where ordinary dependence on water would halt movement?
- trace:
  - 88:17 **يَنظُرُونَ** ن ظ ر B001: Reflective looking asks the reader to infer function from the creature's observable life.
  - 88:17 **ٱلْإِبِلِ** ء ب ل B002: Doing without approaching water supplies the camel's specific ecological constraint and capacity.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B005: Aptness and preparedness recast createdness as fitness for that demanding condition.

## caretakers gaze
- reading: The ayah calls for a caretaker's patient gaze, attentive to both bodily form and living disposition.
- mechanism: The requested gaze is not detached spectatorship but the sustained attention of one who guards, compares condition over time, and knows an animal through care.
- trace:
  - 88:17 **يَنظُرُونَ** ن ظ ر B009: The watcher who looks and guards gives the gaze a responsible, diagnostic posture.
  - 88:17 **ٱلْإِبِلِ** ء ب ل B001: Camel tending supplies the practical field in which such watchful knowledge is acquired.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B004: Inner disposition extends the object of care from outward anatomy to behavior and temperament.

## bundled burden system
- reading: The camel becomes an integrated bundle whose created proportions solve the problem of carrying weight.
- mechanism: The animal can be provisionally modeled as a bundled load-bearing system: many coordinated members are bound into one form capable of receiving and moving weight.
- trace:
  - 88:17 **يَنظُرُونَ** ن ظ ر B006: Matching counterpart with counterpart supplies an analogical way to compare the animal's coordinated members.
  - 88:17 **ٱلْإِبِلِ** ء ب ل B004: Weight and liability introduce the load that the creature's form must bear.
  - 88:17 **ٱلْإِبِلِ** ء ب ل B005: The bound bundle supplies a material analogy for plurality held together as one carrying unit.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B001: Measurement constrains the bundle analogy to proportioned load-bearing design.

## arrival return gait
- reading: Look at the camel in motion, especially the measured recurrence by which its limbs carry it through a journey.
- mechanism: The surah's arrival and return vocabulary activates a dynamic rather than static camel: a camel-specific return of the forelegs at the opening and recurring limbs in travel near the close make gait itself an answer to how it was proportioned.
- trace:
  - 88:17 **ٱلْإِبِلِ** ء ب ل B001: The focus camel supplies the moving body to which the two travel images can return.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B001: Proportioning makes repeated limb motion a created mechanical coordination.
  - 88:1 **أَتَىٰكَ** ء ت ي B009: The return of a she-camel's forelegs in travel supplies an unexpectedly exact image of cyclical gait.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B003: Recurring limbs in journeying extends the opening foreleg image into a full motion cycle.

## gaze against covering
- reading: The audience is asked to reverse a covered, averted orientation and let an ordinary visible creature disclose what has been refused.
- mechanism: The focus gaze occupies a field of opposed orientations: an overwhelming cover, a cover over understanding, turning the face away, and covering truth. Looking at the camel therefore becomes an act of uncovering and reorientation, not bare retinal exposure.
- trace:
  - 88:17 **يَنظُرُونَ** ن ظ ر B001: Directed sight and insight provide the positive act opposed to concealment and aversion.
  - 88:1 **ٱلْغَٰشِيَةِ** غ ش و B002: A covering that encompasses everything supplies the opening condition through which attention must work.
  - 88:1 **ٱلْغَٰشِيَةِ** غ ش و B007: The non-dominant mapped root's covering of understanding internalizes the obstruction as cognitive occlusion.
  - 88:23 **تَوَلَّىٰ** و ل ي B007: Turning away supplies the directional refusal that reverses the focus command to turn attention toward.
  - 88:23 **وَكَفَرَ** ك ف ر B003: Veiling truth makes non-recognition an active covering rather than a shortage of visible evidence.

## anti futility ecology
- reading: The camel answers the earlier anti-ecology with a created system in which bodily capacities make scarce inputs sustain labor and travel.
- mechanism: The punitive sequence is an ecology whose inputs fail: exhausting work, imposed drink, forage that does not nourish, and hunger that remains. Against it, the camel's water independence and fitness become a compact countermodel of created capacities that actually sustain work and movement.
- trace:
  - 88:17 **ٱلْإِبِلِ** ء ب ل B002: The camel's ability to do without approaching water supplies resilience missing from the failed punitive ecology.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B005: Aptness makes resilience a fitted capacity rather than accidental endurance.
  - 88:3 **نَّاصِبَةٌ** ن ص ب B004: Exhaustion and wearing toil state the failure condition that a viable burden-bearer must resist.
  - 88:5 **تُسْقَىٰ** س ق ي B001: Giving drink introduces hydration as an externally controlled input.
  - 88:6 **ضَرِيعٍ** ض ر ع B005: The named forage plant supplies food material that is present yet functionally barren.
  - 88:7 **يُسْمِنُ** س م ن B001: Fatness versus emaciation provides a visible measure of whether food has become bodily reserve.
  - 88:7 **يُغْنِى** غ ن ي B002: Sufficiency names the functional outcome explicitly denied to the punitive food.
  - 88:7 **جُوعٍ** ج و ع B001: An empty belly completes the causal chain of failed intake and failed sustenance.

## fire contains camel ecology
- reading: The camel invitation can disclose a buried reversal in the fire scene: pasture normally enters and sustains the camel, whereas fire encloses and consumes its sufferers.
- mechanism: The fire verse unexpectedly carries camel material inside both of its verbal branches: two mapped roots offer pasture grazed by camels, while the heat root offers a camel category. The later focus camel can therefore act as a retrospective key that reverses an earlier grazing ecology into an ecology of consuming fire.
- trace:
  - 88:17 **ٱلْإِبِلِ** ء ب ل B001: The explicit camel and its tending resolve the earlier camel-specific lexical images into one possible latent field.
  - 88:4 **تَصْلَىٰ** ص ل ي B010: A plant grazed by camels places ordinary feeding inside the branch inventory of contact with fire.
  - 88:4 **تَصْلَىٰ** ص ل ي B009: The non-dominant mapped root independently preserves the same camel-pasture image and keeps the split mapping live.
  - 88:4 **حَامِيَةً** ح م ي B005: The protected camel category makes camel vocabulary latent even in the adjective describing the fire's heat.

## hump between collapse and erection
- reading: The camel becomes a middle-scale study in how a burdened body is formed to preserve elevation between bodily collapse and mountain-like erection.
- mechanism: A vanished hump or crest images humiliation, exhausting labor bends the body, and the later mountain is erected as a hard elevated mass. The camel's visible hump and burden-bearing body can occupy the middle: a created geometry that remains raised while carrying weight.
- trace:
  - 88:17 **يَنظُرُونَ** ن ظ ر B006: Counterpart comparison licenses a controlled visual analogy among lowered face, camel form, and erected mountain.
  - 88:17 **ٱلْإِبِلِ** ء ب ل B004: Weight and burden give the camel's raised form a load-bearing problem to solve.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B003: Complete visible form anchors the analogy in morphology rather than treating the camel as an independent symbol.
  - 88:2 **خَٰشِعَةٌ** خ ش ع B004: A hump whose crest has gone supplies a startling bodily image for abject loss of elevation.
  - 88:3 **نَّاصِبَةٌ** ن ص ب B004: Exhausting toil supplies the downward pressure exerted on a working body.
  - 88:19 **نُصِبَتْ** ن ص ب B001: Erecting something prominently reverses the earlier exhaustion image into maintained uprightness.
  - 88:19 **ٱلْجِبَالِ** ج ب ل B001: A hard elevated mass supplies the mountain pole of the visual comparison.

## eye spring water route
- reading: The looking eye, the flowing spring, and the camel that can travel without nearing water form a single inquiry into how life is fitted to water.
- mechanism: The organ of looking and the water source share one branch field, while springs appear once as imposed boiling drink and once as flowing abundance. The water-independent camel makes the gaze travel along this eye-spring ambiguity from seeing water to understanding movement through its presence or absence.
- trace:
  - 88:17 **يَنظُرُونَ** ن ظ ر B008: The eye as the place of sight supplies the bodily instrument that begins the water-reading route.
  - 88:17 **ٱلْإِبِلِ** ء ب ل B002: Doing without approaching water lets the camel carry the hydrological comparison beyond the spring itself.
  - 88:5 **تُسْقَىٰ** س ق ي B001: Being given drink supplies the first, coercive relation between body and water.
  - 88:5 **عَيْنٍ** ع ي ن B006: The water source supplies the spring pole of the eye-spring ambiguity in the punitive scene.
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B001: The seeing eye keeps perception active inside the later garden spring.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B001: Flowing and coursing convert the second spring from a static source into available movement.

## livestock labor fulfillment
- reading: The camel is a created partner in a provision system where skilled tending and fitted capacity can make striving fruitful rather than futile.
- mechanism: The pleasant face's good condition contains a direct livestock-and-camels branch and is satisfied with its striving. This activates the focus camel as a nexus where tending, animal wealth, useful labor, and fulfilled outcome meet, contrasting with the earlier exhausted labor.
- trace:
  - 88:17 **ٱلْإِبِلِ** ء ب ل B001: Camels, herds, ownership, and skill in tending supply a concrete economy of sustained work.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B005: Fitness makes the animal a capable participant in that economy rather than inert wealth.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B001: Good condition and beneficence supply the fulfilled state associated with the pleasant face.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B005: Livestock and camels place the focus animal directly inside the branch field of that good condition.
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B002: Work, earning, and practical activity supply the human effort joined to the animal's tending.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B001: Acceptance rather than displeasure marks the effort as reaching a satisfying outcome.

## accessible affordance
- reading: The camel's createdness includes how a tall, powerful body becomes calmly available for approach, mounting, and use.
- mechanism: The garden objects are placed, aligned, and spread for use; within that sequence, the placing root explicitly includes a camel lowering its neck for mounting. The focus question can therefore concern affordance: how a large creature is made accessible to a human rider.
- trace:
  - 88:17 **ٱلْإِبِلِ** ء ب ل B001: The tended camel supplies the living user-facing object whose accessibility is in question.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B005: Aptness and preparation turn lowering for a rider into a created affordance.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B001: Setting an object in a prescribed lower place establishes the surrounding pattern of readiness for use.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B013: A camel lowering its neck for mounting gives that readiness a precise camel-specific bodily action.
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: Alignment on an even line adds orderly placement to the usable environment.
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B001: Spreading objects distributes access across the scene rather than concentrating it at one point.

## collective arrangement
- reading: The ayah can also ask how camels are created as a relational collective that alternates between ordered following and distributed spread.
