- mechanism: Context supplies both threatening envelopment and a garden defined by protective concealment. Covering is therefore not intrinsically evil; the focus act is culpable because it covers truth or favor, not merely because it performs the material operation of concealment.
- trace:
  - 88:23 **وَكَفَرَ** ك ف ر B001: Neutral material covering provides the shared operation whose value is tested by the surrounding enclosures.
  - 88:23 **وَكَفَرَ** ك ف ر B003: Covering truth identifies the focus act's harmful object and prevents a morally neutral reading.
  - 88:1 **ٱلْغَٰشِيَةِ** غ ش و B001: An overlying cover supplies the threatening enclosure at the surah's opening.
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B003: A garden hidden by its trees supplies a beneficial enclosure and separates covering from its moral use.
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B008: Protective shielding confirms that concealment can preserve rather than deny.

## sensory withdrawal exposed
- reading: It is withdrawal of face, hearing, insight, and compliance from a claim whose truth is then covered; the later lowered face exposes the orientation that withdrawal tried to hide.
- mechanism: Faces, compliant hearing, and directed looking turn the focus withdrawal into a coordinated shutdown of perceptual and responsive orientation. The humbled face also makes an inward refusal externally legible: what was turned away is eventually shown on the body's front.
- trace:
  - 88:23 **تَوَلَّىٰ** و ل ي B006: Facing with the senses supplies the receptive orientation that the focus person reallocates.
  - 88:23 **تَوَلَّىٰ** و ل ي B007: Averting oneself supplies the refusal across bodily attention and compliance.
  - 88:23 **وَكَفَرَ** ك ف ر B003: Truth-rejection gives the sensory withdrawal an epistemic object rather than reducing it to posture.
  - 88:2 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B001: The face as a person's front makes orientation visible on the body.
  - 88:2 **خَٰشِعَةٌ** خ ش ع B001: Lowered submission supplies the eventual posture that reverses earlier aversion.
  - 88:11 **تَسْمَعُ** س م ع B003: Hearing as understanding and compliance extends turning away from sight to responsive comprehension.
  - 88:17 **يَنظُرُونَ** ن ظ ر B001: Directed sight or insight supplies the positive act refused by selective reorientation.

## captured office
- reading: It can also expose captured authority: someone assumes charge, gates access to truth, and produces toil that remains subject to an accounting outside that office.
- mechanism: A chain of branch images for holding office, administering a group, coercive oversight, exhausting labor, and inspecting an affair activates the focus authority branch. The subject can be modeled as a gatekeeper who takes charge and covers truth, while the surrounding discourse distinguishes that captured office from reminder and final accounting.
- trace:
  - 88:23 **تَوَلَّىٰ** و ل ي B003: Assuming charge supplies the office that can be captured or misused.
  - 88:23 **وَكَفَرَ** ك ف ر B003: Covering or rejecting truth supplies the gatekeeping act performed through that office.
  - 88:3 **عَامِلَةٌ** ع م ل B003: Holding and managing an office directly echoes the focus root's authority branch.
  - 88:3 **نَّاصِبَةٌ** ن ص ب B004: Exhaustion and wearing toil supply the failed output of misdirected administration.
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B003: Administration over a people broadens the repeated office vocabulary from individual work to social governance.
  - 88:22 **بِمُصَيْطِرٍ** س ط ر B003: The coercive overseer supplies the nearby negative limit on human control.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B006: Inspection of an affair supplies a final review function that does not belong to the self-appointed gatekeeper.

## ingratitude toward visible gifts
- reading: It becomes perceptual ingratitude: turning from available provision and constructed signs, then covering their claim to be recognized as favor.
- mechanism: The paired scenes of failed nourishment and abundant ease, followed by directed observation of created forms, provide concrete objects for the focus ingratitude branch. The person does not merely reject a proposition; the person turns from perceptible provision and covers its status as favor and sign.
- trace:
  - 88:23 **تَوَلَّىٰ** و ل ي B007: Withdrawal supplies the refusal to remain attentive to provision and visible construction.
  - 88:23 **وَكَفَرَ** ك ف ر B004: Failure to acknowledge a favor makes ingratitude the specific mode of covering.
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B006: A flowing water source supplies a concrete provision that can be received or denied as favor.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B001: Good condition and blessing name the positive state whose acknowledgment is at stake.
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B003: The tree-enclosed garden gathers the surrounding provisions into a habitable gift.
  - 88:17 **يَنظُرُونَ** ن ظ ر B001: Directed vision or insight makes acknowledgment an act of attending to available evidence.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B002: Creative bringing-into-being specifies what the visible forms are evidence of.

## exception reallocates jurisdiction
- reading: It means 'but/as for the one who withdraws and rejects': the case is identified, human coercion remains excluded, and consequence passes to divine jurisdiction.
- mechanism: The repeated negation and exception pattern, followed by the adjacent reminder-versus-controller distinction and a new divine subject, makes إِلَّا a jurisdictional hinge. The focus clause opens the refuser's case without granting the human reminder coercive control; the next clause transfers consequence to God.
- trace:
  - 88:23 **تَوَلَّىٰ** و ل ي B007: Withdrawal identifies the case that the hinge routes onward rather than the person over whom human control is restored.
  - 88:23 **وَكَفَرَ** ك ف ر B003: Truth-rejection completes the case description before agency shifts in the following clause.
  - 88:6 **لَّيْسَ** ل ي س B002: Exception after negation supplies an earlier syntactic analogue for isolating a remainder without erasing the negation.
  - 88:21 **فَذَكِّرْ مُذَكِّرٌ** ذ ك ر B009: Reminder defines the messenger's positive function immediately before coercive control is denied.
  - 88:22 **لَّسْتَ** ل ي س B001: Present-state negation keeps the denial of human control operative across the focus hinge.
  - 88:22 **بِمُصَيْطِرٍ** س ط ر B003: The coercive overseer specifies the role that the preceding negation withholds.
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B005: Punitive consequence supplies the action reached after the focus case is stated.
  - 88:24 **ٱللَّهُ** ء ل ه B001: The divine referent supplies the new agent of consequence and completes the jurisdiction transfer.

## return cancels exit
- reading: The turn is only an outbound leg: return cancels spatial escape, and accounting cancels the attempted social or epistemic disavowal.
- mechanism: The closing return and accounting reverse both verbs of the focus clause. Withdrawal fails to become escape because it bends into return; disavowal fails to dissolve relation because every act remains countable.
- trace:
  - 88:23 **تَوَلَّىٰ** و ل ي B007: Turning away supplies the apparent outbound motion that the closing return reverses.
  - 88:23 **وَكَفَرَ** ك ف ر B005: Disavowal supplies the attempted severance that accounting refuses to treat as a vanished relation.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return to a destination folds the person's apparent exit back toward the speaker's domain.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B001: Counting and accounting preserve the acts as liabilities even after the person disowns the bond.

## seed cover after rain
- reading: As a contained ecological counter-image, delayed rain and seed-covering show that some hiddenness precedes emergence, thereby making the focus act a morally perverted use of an operation that can also nurture.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:23 **تَوَلَّىٰ** و ل ي B010: Rain arriving after an earlier rain supplies delayed succession and latent renewal within the focus root.
  - 88:23 **وَكَفَرَ** ك ف ر B008: A cultivator covering seed supplies concealment as an enabling material act rather than annihilation.
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B011: Entwined, thrusting vegetation supplies the later emergence latent in covered seed.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B001: Flowing movement supplies the water path connecting delayed rain to growth.
  - 88:20 **ٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B002: Soft productive earth supplies the receiving medium for the covered seed.
  - 88:20 **سُطِحَتْ** س ط ح B006: Vegetation spreading over the ground supplies the visible outcome of initially hidden growth.

## crowned counter authority
- reading: As a contained political image, concealment becomes the officeholder's crown: the subject self-authorizes through what is covered, while context denies that counter-sovereignty final control.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:23 **تَوَلَّىٰ** و ل ي B003: Taking charge supplies accession to authority.
  - 88:23 **وَكَفَرَ** ك ف ر B015: A crown that covers supplies the image of concealment worn as authorization.
  - 88:10 **عَالِيَةٍ** ع ل و B003: Greatness shading into self-exaltation supplies the vertical ambition of the counter-authority.
  - 88:22 **بِمُصَيْطِرٍ** س ط ر B003: Coercive oversight supplies the power-form explicitly denied to the human reminder.
  - 88:24 **ٱلْأَكْبَرَ** ك ب ر B006: Majesty and greatness relocate ultimate scale away from the self-crowned subject.

## under saddle burden
- reading: As a contained camel-load image, the covered burden remains in transmission beneath the journey and comes back with the returning traveler.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:23 **تَوَلَّىٰ** و ل ي B011: The cloth beneath a saddle supplies a hidden interface that receives and transmits load.
  - 88:23 **وَكَفَرَ** ك ف ر B001: Literal covering makes the burden-bearing layer invisible without removing its pressure.
  - 88:17 **ٱلْإِبِلِ** ء ب ل B004: Burden and liability in the camel field supply what the hidden layer continues to carry.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B003: Repeated bodily movement in travel and return supplies the route across which the concealed load persists.


# Focus 88:24

## penal intensity
- reading: The ayah stages a fully assigned penal event: Allah actively makes the referent undergo punishment, names that condition again, and grades it as the greater one.
- mechanism: The repeated ع ذ ب root turns punishment into both an enacted process and a named result; the divine name fixes the agent, while أَكْبَرَ ranks the result by degree.
- trace:
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B005: Torment and severe punishment supply the direct penal sense, while the verb-noun doubling makes it both act and resulting condition.
  - 88:24 **ٱللَّهُ** ء ل ه B002: The fixed divine name literally identifies Allah and assigns the punishing agency to Him.
  - 88:24 **ٱلْأَكْبَرَ** ك ب ر B001: Greatness as the opposite of smallness supplies the scale on which the punishment is ranked.

## withholding regime
- reading: A coexisting reading hears punishment as an imposed deprivation regime: sweetness, intake, and relief are withheld until abstention itself becomes torment.
- mechanism: The causative verb can be heard alongside the root's withholding and weaning branch: punishment is not only pain added to a body but access to relief actively removed from it.
- trace:
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B001: Fresh palatability supplies the absent good whose loss gives deprivation a sensory edge.
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B002: Bodily abstention from food and drink supplies the somatic state into which the punished person may be driven.
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B003: Withholding and weaning supply the operative mechanism of removing access rather than merely adding pain.
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B005: Punishment keeps the deprivation model anchored in the actual penal construction rather than replacing it with a free metaphor.

## unbearable weight
- reading: Greater also marks the punishment as the chief, uncarryable burden, a qualitative excess over the sufferer's capacity rather than merely more units of pain.
- mechanism: The ك ب ر inventory shifts the scalar adjective from simple quantity toward heaviness and the bulk of an affair; the punishment is greater because it is harder to bear and occupies the chief burden.
- trace:
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B005: Severe punishment supplies the burden-bearing event that the adjective recalibrates.
  - 88:24 **ٱلْأَكْبَرَ** ك ب ر B002: The chief share or bulk of an affair makes greater denote the dominant portion of the consequence.
  - 88:24 **ٱلْأَكْبَرَ** ك ب ر B010: Greatness as hardship and heaviness converts scalar size into felt difficulty of carrying the punishment.

## counterwage exhaustion
- reading: The punishment can be a counter-wage system: effort continues and grows heavy, but every nourishing or satisfying return from that effort is withheld.
- mechanism: The two face-groups create rival economies of effort: one works into exhaustion, while the other is satisfied with its striving. The focus withholding branch makes the greater punishment legible as labor whose yield, wage, and relief never arrive.
- trace:
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B003: Withholding and weaning turn punishment into the blocking of an expected return from effort.
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B005: Torment keeps the blocked-yield mechanism penal rather than merely economic.
  - 88:24 **ٱلْأَكْبَرَ** ك ب ر B010: Hardship and heaviness make the endless work-load a quality of the greater punishment.
  - 88:3 **عَامِلَةٌ** ع م ل B001: Intentional work supplies productive effort as the first side of the contrast.
  - 88:3 **عَامِلَةٌ** ع م ل B004: The wage or provision of work supplies the expected yield whose absence makes the labor punitive.
  - 88:3 **نَّاصِبَةٌ** ن ص ب B004: Exhausting toil supplies the bodily cost of work severed from recompense.
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B002: Work and earning supply the parallel effort on the satisfied side of the contrast.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B001: Satisfaction opposed to displeasure supplies the successful result withheld from the exhausted workers.

## counterfeit nourishment
- reading: The greater punishment becomes an integrated anti-sustenance system: the victim is made to consume, yet consumption withholds sweetness, nourishment, sufficiency, and the ending of hunger.
- mechanism: The fire, heated spring, forced drink, food, failed fattening, failed sufficiency, and hunger sequence turns the focus root's palatability, abstention, and withholding branches into a closed anti-nourishment circuit. Apparent provision is delivered, but it intensifies the need it should resolve.
- trace:
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B001: Fresh palatability supplies the precise sensory good inverted by the surrounding drink and food.
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B002: Abstention from food and drink supplies the bodily deprivation produced despite the presence of consumables.
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B003: Withholding and weaning provide the causal operation by which relief is denied.
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B005: Torment and punishment anchor the anti-nourishment circuit as a mode of punishment.
  - 88:4 **تَصْلَىٰ** ص ل ي B003: Encountering fire and its heat supplies the bodily environment in which drink cannot cool or relieve.
  - 88:4 **حَامِيَةً** ح م ي B001: Heating supplies the thermal force that converts offered liquid into injury.
  - 88:5 **تُسْقَىٰ** س ق ي B001: Giving drink supplies the literal act of provisioning that the mechanism reverses.
  - 88:5 **تُسْقَىٰ** س ق ي B001: The non-dominant mapped image of driving and expulsion contributes a tentative coercive vector to the passive giving of drink.
  - 88:5 **عَيْنٍ** ع ي ن B006: A flowing-water source supplies the expected origin of refreshment on the punitive side.
  - 88:5 **ءَانِيَةٍ** ء ن ي B003: Reaching full term supplies the liquid's completed, fully arrived condition rather than a passing warmth.
  - 88:6 **طَعَامٌ** ط ع م B001: Tasting and taking food supply ingestion without successful nourishment.
  - 88:7 **يُسْمِنُ** س م ن B001: Fatness opposed to emaciation supplies the bodily result that the food explicitly fails to produce.
  - 88:7 **يُغْنِى** غ ن ي B002: Sufficiency supplies the functional relief that the food explicitly fails to provide.
  - 88:7 **جُوعٍ** ج و ع B001: An empty belly supplies the unresolved need left intact after eating.
  - 88:12 **عَيْنٌ** ع ي ن B006: The second spring repeats the water-source image on the contrasting side, where it can function as genuine provision.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B001: Continuous flowing supplies an unobstructed provision pattern opposed to punitive withholding.

## cosmic load scale
- reading: Greater acquires architectural weight: the punishment is an uncarryable load heard against the same agency's raising, erecting, and spreading of structures from couch to cosmos.
- mechanism: Raised garden space, elevated couches, raised sky, erected mountains, and spread earth provide a sequence of load-bearing scales. Against that sequence, أَكْبَرَ can mark a punishment whose heaviness exceeds creaturely carrying capacity while remaining within the agency that orders world-scale structures.
- trace:
  - 88:24 **ٱلْأَكْبَرَ** ك ب ر B001: Greatness supplies the explicit scalar anchor that the spatial sequence can recalibrate.
  - 88:24 **ٱلْأَكْبَرَ** ك ب ر B010: Hardship and heaviness convert visible scale into an unbearable load on the punished person.
  - 88:10 **عَالِيَةٍ** ع ل و B001: Elevation supplies the first explicit vertical scale in the favorable scene.
  - 88:13 **مَّرْفُوعَةٌ** ر ف ع B001: Raising an object supplies a human-scale instance of ordered elevation.
  - 88:18 **ٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B004: The sky as what rises and overshadows supplies the world-scale upper structure.
  - 88:18 **رُفِعَتْ** ر ف ع B001: The sky's raising repeats elevation at a scale far beyond the couches.
  - 88:19 **ٱلْجِبَالِ** ج ب ل B001: A high solid mass supplies visible weight and load-bearing resistance.
  - 88:19 **نُصِبَتْ** ن ص ب B001: Setting something upright and prominent supplies the mountains' imposed stability.
  - 88:20 **ٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B001: The lower realm opposite the sky supplies the other bound of the spatial scale.
  - 88:20 **سُطِحَتْ** س ط ح B001: A level extended surface supplies ordered breadth in contrast to vertical raising.

## evidentiary threshold
- reading: It becomes the terminal consequence of an epistemic sequence: report, inspection, reminder, deliberate covering, and final examination, with greater registering cumulative refusal as well as severity.
- mechanism: A report arrives, perception is directed toward measurable creation, reminder reactivates what can be known, concealment blocks it, and examination closes the sequence. The initial فَ of فَيُعَذِّبُهُ then reads as a consequence after multiple modes of disclosure rather than an unprepared threat.
- trace:
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B005: Punishment supplies the consequence whose evidentiary position is being revised.
  - 88:24 **ٱلْأَكْبَرَ** ك ب ر B002: The chief burden of the affair lets greater register the accumulated weight of refused disclosure.
  - 88:1 **أَتَىٰكَ** ء ت ي B001: Coming and arrival supply the first movement of information toward the addressee.
  - 88:1 **حَدِيثُ** ح د ث B003: Renewed speech as report supplies an audible disclosure channel.
  - 88:17 **يَنظُرُونَ** ن ظ ر B001: Directing sight or insight supplies deliberate examination rather than passive exposure.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B001: Proportioning and measuring supply an intelligible order available to inspection.
  - 88:21 **فَذَكِّرْ مُذَكِّرٌ** ذ ك ر B003: Bringing something back after forgetfulness supplies renewed cognitive access.
  - 88:23 **وَكَفَرَ** ك ف ر B003: Concealing the truth supplies an active obstruction after disclosure.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B006: Examining a matter supplies the closing review that makes the sequence accountable.

## jurisdictional handoff
- reading: The focus instead seals a division of authority: the messenger reminds but does not control; the refuser chooses turning and concealment; Allah alone punishes, receives the return, and accounts.
- mechanism: The reminder is assigned to the human addressee, control is explicitly negated, refusal is assigned to the one who turns away and covers, and punishment, return, and accounting are assigned to Allah. The focus is therefore a jurisdictional transfer, not an extension of human coercive authority.
- trace:
  - 88:24 **ٱللَّهُ** ء ل ه B002: The fixed divine name explicitly assigns the punitive act to Allah.
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B005: Punishment supplies the sanction whose proper agent and place in the sequence are delimited.
  - 88:21 **فَذَكِّرْ مُذَكِّرٌ** ذ ك ر B009: Reminder as what makes one remember supplies the messenger's positively assigned function.
  - 88:22 **لَّسْتَ** ل ي س B001: Negation of a present state supplies the explicit removal of coercive status.
  - 88:22 **بِمُصَيْطِرٍ** س ط ر B003: The controlling overseer supplies the authority specifically denied to the reminder.
  - 88:23 **تَوَلَّىٰ** و ل ي B007: Turning away supplies the addressee's own refusal rather than an act produced by the reminder.
  - 88:23 **وَكَفَرَ** ك ف ر B003: Concealing the truth supplies the accountable obstruction attributed to the refuser.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return to a destination supplies the movement back into divine jurisdiction.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B001: Counting and accounting supply the final adjudicative function assigned to the divine side.

## orientation reversal
- reading: Punishment is also the hinge of a failed escape: the self turns its presented face away, but return reverses the motion and makes encounter unavoidable.
- mechanism: The two face tableaux establish persons by their presented fronts, turning away reverses that orientation, and return cancels the attempted distance. The focus punishment occupies the hinge where an evasive motion becomes an unavoidable re-encounter.
- trace:
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B005: Punishment supplies the consequential hinge between turning away and compelled return.
  - 88:24 **ٱللَّهُ** ء ل ه B002: The divine name supplies the destination and agent whom attempted distancing cannot evade.
  - 88:2 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B004: The face standing for the self supplies the person as a visibly oriented whole.
  - 88:8 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B001: The face and forward aspect repeat orientation on the contrasting outcome.
  - 88:23 **تَوَلَّىٰ** و ل ي B006: Turning the face and approaching supply the positive orientation whose reversal is available in the same root.
  - 88:23 **تَوَلَّىٰ** و ل ي B007: Turning the back and withdrawing supply the actual evasive motion immediately before the focus.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return to the destination supplies the reversal that closes the attempted distance.

## unsheltered exposure
- reading: It is also radical unsheltering: the one who covers is stripped of every protective cover and left exposed beneath the very sky invoked in the focus root's distant branch.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B004: Being open to the sky supplies forced exposure as the outlier's focus-side material image.
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B005: Torment and punishment keep exposure subordinate to the penal construction.
  - 88:1 **ٱلْغَٰشِيَةِ** غ ش و B001: A cover rising over and concealing a thing supplies the opening state against which exposure reverses.
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B001: Sheltering and concealment supply the protected alternative associated with the garden scene.
  - 88:18 **ٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B004: The sky as what rises and overshadows supplies the cover from which the focus branch imagines removal.
  - 88:23 **وَكَفَرَ** ك ف ر B001: Covering supplies the refuser's own concealment, which the exposure reading then reverses.

## beyond camel capacity
- reading: As a contained material analogy, the punishment exceeds even the imagined conjunction of thirst endurance and burden-bearing: it is deprivation and load beyond adaptive capacity.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B002: Abstention from food and drink supplies the endurance stress carried by the focus root.
  - 88:24 **ٱلْأَكْبَرَ** ك ب ر B010: Hardship and heaviness supply the load whose excess defines greater.
  - 88:17 **ٱلْإِبِلِ** ء ب ل B002: Making do without water supplies a visible creaturely model of adaptation to abstention.
  - 88:17 **ٱلْإِبِلِ** ء ب ل B004: Weight and liability supply the same creature's burden-bearing capacity.
  - 88:5 **تُسْقَىٰ** س ق ي B001: Giving drink supplies the immediate deprivation domain against which the camel's water endurance is measured.

## punitive emergence
- reading: In a tightly contained outlier, punishment resembles a second, harsher emergence: concealment breaks, the burden is laid down, and the subject is delivered into unavoidable return and accounting.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B009: Womb and afterbirth supply a material passage from enclosure to exposed life.
  - 88:24 **ٱلْأَكْبَرَ** ك ب ر B008: Birth-order seniority supplies a distant way for greater to mark an emergence ranked beyond an earlier birth.
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B007: The fetus hidden in the belly supplies protected gestation before emergence.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B002: Laying down a burden through childbirth supplies the transition out of enclosure.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B002: Bringing creation into existence supplies the emergence frame at creaturely scale.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return to the destination turns emergence into arrival before accountability.

## contest of greatness
- reading: A coexisting relational reading hears it as the overcoming of false self-magnification: attempted independence turns into abasement before the rightful divine greatness.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:24 **ٱلْأَكْبَرَ** ك ب ر B006: Majesty, self-exaltation, and refusal of truth supply competing postures of greatness.
  - 88:24 **ٱلْأَكْبَرَ** ك ب ر B011: Contending and overcoming supply the directional result in which one claim to greatness defeats another.
  - 88:24 **ٱللَّهُ** ء ل ه B002: The fixed divine name supplies the rightful agent beside whom the comparative adjective stands.
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B005: Punishment supplies the concrete means by which the posture of self-exaltation is overcome.
  - 88:2 **خَٰشِعَةٌ** خ ش ع B001: Lowering and submission supply the reversed posture produced on the punished side.
  - 88:23 **تَوَلَّىٰ** و ل ي B007: Turning away supplies refusal as a posture of attempted independence.
  - 88:23 **وَكَفَرَ** ك ف ر B003: Concealing truth supplies the resisted claim that the greater punishment answers.


# Focus 88:25

## return as gathering point
- reading: Their return is a collective convergence whose very return-place is with Us.
- mechanism: The return-place branch lets إياب name both movement back and the point at which dispersed persons gather. Fronting إِلَيْنَا makes the destination constitutive of the event: this is not motion in general but their convergence upon Us.
- trace:
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return to a return-place supplies both the homeward motion and its terminal gathering function.

## many quarters one destination
- reading: However divergent their quarters and orientations, every line of arrival closes upon Us.
- mechanism: The quarter-or-direction branch keeps the plurality spatially live: their arrivals may issue from many orientations, yet the fronted إِلَيْنَا collapses those quarters into one endpoint.
- trace:
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B004: The image of a quarter from which one comes turns the plural return into convergence from multiple directions.

## rhythmic return
- reading: Their return gathers the repeated motions and exertions by which their whole course was traveled.
- mechanism: The branch of limbs repeatedly returning in travel expands إياب into accumulated back-and-forth exertion. The final return gathers a history of repeated bodily motions, not merely a single last reversal.
- trace:
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B003: Rhythmic return of limbs in travel gives the return an iterative, embodied interior.

## arrival meets return
- reading: The line completes a reciprocal directional frame: what comes upon the hearer announces the return of its human subjects to Us.
- mechanism: The opening supplies an arrival toward the addressee and, through the non-dominant mapped branch of غ ش و, an approach that reaches its target. Against those incoming vectors, إيابهم becomes the answering vector: the event comes upon its hearers, and the persons implicated in it return to Us.
- trace:
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return to a gathering point supplies the humanward vector that answers the opening arrival.
  - 88:1 **أَتَىٰكَ** ء ت ي B001: Coming and arrival establish the first directed motion, toward the addressee.
  - 88:1 **ٱلْغَٰشِيَةِ** غ ش و B005: The non-dominant coming-or-visitation image makes the covering event an arrival that reaches those addressed.

## common return differentiates faces
- reading: Their common return is a shared confrontation that preserves and exposes sharply different lived trajectories.
- mechanism: Two sets of faces occupy opposed conditions, while one set is explicitly satisfied with its striving. Their shared return therefore does not flatten them into an undifferentiated mass; convergence becomes a facing encounter in which distinct histories become manifest.
- trace:
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: The gathering-point image supplies the common endpoint shared by the contrasted faces.
  - 88:2 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B003: Facing and confrontation turn arrival at the endpoint into an encounter rather than mere relocation.
  - 88:2 **خَٰشِعَةٌ** خ ش ع B001: Lowering and submission supply one visibly manifested condition at the encounter.
  - 88:8 **نَّاعِمَةٌ** ن ع م B001: Good condition and favor supply the opposed manifested condition without changing the common endpoint.
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B001: Purposeful movement toward an objective gives the differentiated face a prior trajectory.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B001: Acceptance rather than displeasure marks one trajectory as affirmatively recognized at return.

## exhaustion to emplacement
- reading: Return converts exhausted, repeated motion into a final placement as settled and conspicuous as something erected.
- mechanism: The same context root spans exhausted human labor and a mountain set upright. Coupled with the focus branches of traveled recurrence and return-place, this creates a motion-to-fixity model: spent movement ends in a definitive emplacement.
- trace:
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B003: Repeated bodily movement supplies the mobile, effort-bearing side of the transition.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: The return-place supplies the fixed terminus at which motion is gathered.
  - 88:3 **نَّاصِبَةٌ** ن ص ب B004: Weariness and affliction make the prior motion costly and depleting.
  - 88:19 **نُصِبَتْ** ن ص ب B001: Setting something upright and conspicuous supplies the contrasting image of final fixed placement.

## return place as prepared order
- reading: For the favorable trajectory, return is reception into a destination already positioned, aligned, spread, and made fit for repose.
- mechanism: The favorable scene is not empty space: concealment by growth, resting places, objects put in position, aligned cushions, and spread furnishings make a prepared spatial order. For this coexisting trajectory, the return-place branch becomes reception into an already arranged habitation.
- trace:
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return-place and gathering point provide the destination that the material sequence renders habitable.
  - 88:10 **جَنَّةٍ** ج ن ن B003: A garden screened by growth supplies a bounded, inhabited destination rather than an abstract elsewhere.
  - 88:13 **سُرُرٌ** س ر ر B011: A place of settled rest turns arrival into repose and emplacement.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B001: Putting an object in its appointed place supplies deliberate preparation before the returners arrive.
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: Alignment on an even line gives the destination visible order.
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B001: Dispersal across the space fills out the prepared field into which gathering can occur.

## directed looking culminates
- reading: إِلَيْنَا completes a practiced redirection: every inspected quarter becomes a line of orientation whose ultimate closure is toward Us.
- mechanism: The fourfold direction of attention toward camel, sky, mountains, and earth rehearses movement along visible quarters. Creation, raising, erection, and surfacing make those quarters legible as made order; the fronted إِلَيْنَا then reads as the culminating direction beyond the inspected field.
- trace:
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B004: The quarter from which one comes lets the inspected directions become multiple departure lines for one final return.
  - 88:17 **يَنظُرُونَ** ن ظ ر B001: Directing sight or insight supplies the repeated act of orientation.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B002: Bringing creation into being makes the first inspected object evidence of originated order.
  - 88:18 **رُفِعَتْ** ر ف ع B001: Raising supplies the upward axis of the inspected field.
  - 88:19 **نُصِبَتْ** ن ص ب B001: Erecting something visibly supplies a fixed vertical marker within that field.
  - 88:20 **سُطِحَتْ** س ط ح B001: An extended level surface supplies the horizontal axis and closes the directional survey.

## reminder without custody
- reading: To Us assigns exclusive custody of their return, sharply separating divine ingathering from the messenger's non-coercive reminder.
- mechanism: Remembering and reminder belong to the messenger's task, while controlling surveillance is explicitly negated. The fronted إِلَيْنَا consequently marks exclusive custody: speech can recall and direct, but it does not own, force, or administer the return.
- trace:
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: The return-place with Us supplies the jurisdiction withheld from the human reminder.
  - 88:21 **فَذَكِّرْ مُذَكِّرٌ** ذ ك ر B003: Restoring something to awareness defines the messenger's limited, non-coercive operation.
  - 88:22 **لَّسْتَ** ل ي س B001: Present-state negation sharply excludes the attributed role of control.
  - 88:22 **بِمُصَيْطِرٍ** س ط ر B003: The image of a dominating overseer names precisely the custody denied to the messenger.

## turning away is reversed
- reading: Even deliberate aversion and concealment remain bounded motions: turning away cannot furnish an alternate final direction.
- mechanism: Turning away gives the person a chosen outward vector, and covering truth makes that vector self-concealing. The return-place branch reverses both claims: aversion can determine present orientation but cannot establish another terminal quarter.
- trace:
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return to the gathering point supplies the unavoidable terminal vector.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B004: The quarter-of-coming image denies turning-away the power to create an independent final direction.
  - 88:23 **تَوَلَّىٰ** و ل ي B007: Turning the back and withdrawing supplies the deliberate vector that the focus overturns.
  - 88:23 **وَكَفَرَ** ك ف ر B003: Concealing truth makes the away-turn an attempted withdrawal from recognition as well as direction.

## return hands into account
- reading: Return is the decisive transfer into divine custody; account is the distinct counting and review that lies upon Us thereafter.
- mechanism: The focus stands between divine punishment and a subsequent account assigned upon Us. Counting and examining the matter distinguish حساب from إياب: return is the transfer into divine custody that makes review inescapable, not merely another word for review.
- trace:
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: The gathering-point image makes return the intake or transfer around which the closing acts are arranged.
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B005: Pain and penalty supply the judicial consequence immediately before the custody clause.
  - 88:24 **ٱللَّهُ** ء ل ه B001: The worshiped deity image identifies the acting authority rather than leaving the first-person plural indeterminate.
  - 88:24 **ٱلْأَكْبَرَ** ك ب ر B002: The weight of the paramount matter enlarges the consequence framing the return.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B001: Counting and accounting supply the distinct operation that follows return.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B006: Examining a matter turns account from raw arithmetic into responsible review.

## human setting at the horizon
- reading: Like a setting that reaches its assigned place, their disappearance can be imaged as the closure of a course in return to Us.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B007: The sun's return to its setting-place supplies a cycle in which vanishing from sight is arrival, not annihilation.
  - 88:18 **ٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B004: The sky as what rises and overshadows supplies the upper field across which a setting course can be imagined.
  - 88:20 **ٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B001: The lower realm opposed to the sky supplies the horizon-like terminal field for the analogy.

## embodied procession
- reading: Their return can be carried as a gathered procession of working limbs, purposeful courses, following tracks, and continuous flow.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B003: Rhythmic return of limbs in travel supplies the embodied cadence of the procession.
  - 88:3 **عَامِلَةٌ** ع م ل B010: The working limb makes bodily action, rather than abstract biography, part of what is gathered.
  - 88:5 **تُسْقَىٰ** س ق ي B012: The non-dominant image of successive following in one track supplies procession and sequence without replacing the source's watering sense.
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B001: Purposeful motion toward an objective gives the procession intention and accumulated striving.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B001: Continuous flow keeps the return processive rather than reducing it to a punctual arrival.

## concealment cannot exit the event
- reading: Their return is the point at which chosen concealment is gathered into an all-reaching event and can no longer secure an outside.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return to a gathering point supplies the collision point between private concealment and comprehensive event.
  - 88:1 **ٱلْغَٰشِيَةِ** غ ش و B002: An event that encompasses and covers all denies the concealer an exterior refuge.
  - 88:1 **حَدِيثُ** ح د ث B006: Bringing a thing forth and making it manifest supplies the counter-motion to concealment.
  - 88:23 **وَكَفَرَ** ك ف ر B003: Concealing truth supplies the person's attempted withdrawal from manifestation.


# Focus 88:26

## complete reckoning
- reading: God takes upon Himself the complete reckoning of each person's case, with nothing delegated or left uncounted.
- mechanism: Counting becomes a complete account of what belongs to each person, while علينا marks custody of both the procedure and its completion.
- trace:
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B001: Counting and reckoning supplies the literal audit image and anchors a complete, proportioned account of them.

## accountable custody
- reading: Their entire accountability is an affair God undertakes, administers, and brings to resolution.
- mechanism: The supervision branch turns reckoning into accountable management: the clause assigns an ongoing jurisdiction and not merely a moment of arithmetic.
- trace:
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B006: Supervision and accountable management supplies the literal administrative image and makes علينا an assignment of jurisdiction.

## sufficient settlement
- reading: The undertaken reckoning is also the sufficient settlement of all that is due from and to them.
- mechanism: Enoughness recasts the account as a settlement sufficient to dispose of every claim concerning them, without replacing the noun's primary reckoning sense.
- trace:
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B003: Sufficiency and enoughness supplies a settling image: the divinely undertaken account is enough to resolve their case.

## estimate overruled
- reading: It also places every estimate, including their own expectations about themselves, under a final divine determination.
- mechanism: The supposition branch creates an epistemic edge: human estimates remain provisional because the decisive reckoning rests with God.
- trace:
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B002: Reckoning as supposition supplies the literal mental-estimate image and lets علينا reserve the conclusive estimate for God.

## report to disclosure
- reading: The final account is the event's evidentiary closure: what arrives first as report becomes fully disclosed, tested, and settled.
- mechanism: The surah opens with an arriving, renewed report of an all-enveloping event and closes with حساب. This activates the information-testing branch of the focus root: the final account is not bare arithmetic but the decisive disclosure and verification of what the covering event gathers.
- trace:
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B010: Seeking and testing information supplies the focus image of an evidentiary inquiry and anchors disclosure in حسابهم.
  - 88:1 **أَتَىٰكَ** ء ت ي B001: Coming and arrival supplies the report's movement into hearing and starts an arrival-to-settlement frame.
  - 88:1 **حَدِيثُ** ح د ث B003: Speech renewed as news supplies an evidentiary report whose contents the closing reckoning finally verifies.
  - 88:1 **ٱلْغَٰشِيَةِ** غ ش و B002: A covering that encompasses everything supplies both total scope and concealment, making the account a counter-movement toward disclosure.

## labor outcome audit
- reading: Reckoning tests what the labor was doing and what it actually yielded; strenuous activity and answerable striving do not count alike.
- mechanism: One sequence joins purposeful work to exhaustion, while the counter-sequence joins earned striving to satisfaction. حسابهم therefore discriminates between mere expenditure of effort and effort whose result can be owned with satisfaction.
- trace:
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B001: Counting and reckoning supplies the comparative audit that can distinguish two outwardly active lives.
  - 88:3 **عَامِلَةٌ** ع م ل B001: Purposeful action supplies real labor rather than inactivity, forcing the account to assess more than whether work occurred.
  - 88:3 **نَّاصِبَةٌ** ن ص ب B004: Exhausting toil and affliction supplies effort's cost while withholding any guarantee that cost itself counts as success.
  - 88:9 **لِّسَعْيِهَا** س ع ي B002: Work, earning, and conduct supplies effort as acquired consequence, giving the positive sequence an accountable object.
  - 88:9 **رَاضِيَةٌ** ر ض و B001: Satisfaction opposed to displeasure supplies the result by which the second striving is recognized as fitting.

## sufficiency test
- reading: The account itself becomes a test and settlement of adequacy: it distinguishes what truly answers a need from what leaves the need intact.
- mechanism: The failed food is explicitly unable to suffice against hunger, whereas the positive scene contains continuously flowing provision. This sharply activates the focus root's enoughness branch: the final حساب settles what truly suffices and exposes provision that only appears to do so.
- trace:
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B003: Sufficiency and enoughness supplies the focus criterion by which an apparent provision is tested and a case is finally settled.
  - 88:6 **طَعَامٌ** ط ع م B004: Provision, livelihood, and good condition supplies food's promised function, which the following negations cancel.
  - 88:7 **يُغْنِى** غ ن ي B002: Sufficiency supplies the context's explicit failed criterion and directly activates enoughness within حساب.
  - 88:7 **جُوعٍ** ج و ع B001: The belly's emptiness supplies the unmet need against which the alleged provision proves insufficient.
  - 88:12 **جَارِيَةٌ** ج ر ي B006: Continuing provision and giving supplies the counter-image of sufficiency that does not terminate at first consumption.

## prepared allocation
- reading: Accountability includes the managed allocation of each result into a prepared order, as exact in placement as the scene's furnishings.
- mechanism: Raised, placed, aligned, and distributed furnishings form an administered environment in which every item has a relation and position. The management branch of حساب shifts from abstract oversight toward allocation: accountability culminates in placing outcomes within a prepared order.
- trace:
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B006: Accountable management supplies the administrative focus image that can assign and maintain an ordered outcome.
  - 88:13 **مَّرْفُوعَةٌ** ر ف ع B001: Raising an object supplies one deliberate placement operation within the prepared environment.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B001: Putting something in an appointed or lower place supplies explicit positional assignment.
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: Alignment on an even line supplies relational order among repeated objects.
  - 88:16 **مَبْثُوثَةٌ** ب ث ث B001: Dispersing and distributing supplies coverage of the space rather than a single isolated placement.

## estimation corrected by signs
- reading: Their reckoning also answers the estimates they formed from visible signs, replacing provisional human inference with conclusive determination.
- mechanism: Directed perception, measured creation, and the non-dominant mapped image of a visible diagnostic sign turn the four observations into an exercise in inference. The supposition branch of حساب then reads as the conclusive correction of estimates formed from signs.
- trace:
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B002: Reckoning as supposition supplies the provisional-estimate image that the final divine حساب can confirm or overturn.
  - 88:17 **يَنظُرُونَ** ن ظ ر B001: Directing sight or insight toward a thing supplies the deliberate evidence-gathering operation.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B001: Measuring and proportioning supplies an intelligible structure from which the observer can reason.
  - 88:18 **ٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B002: The split root's non-dominant image of a visible mark indicating condition supplies a diagnostic-sign reading for what is observed.

## cosmic calibration
- reading: Their account is a calibration within the same measured order that proportions creatures and assigns height, stance, and extent.
- mechanism: Creation is presented through proportioning, raising, erecting, and extending. These ordered operations activate the measure and proportion carried by the focus reckoning branch, so human حساب can be heard as calibration within the same exact order rather than an isolated courtroom sum.
- trace:
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B001: Counting and reckoning, including ordered measure and proportion in its packet scope, supplies the calibration image.
  - 88:17 **خُلِقَتْ** خ ل ق B001: Measuring and proportioning supplies the generative operation that makes created form answerable to an order.
  - 88:18 **رُفِعَتْ** ر ف ع B001: Raising supplies a vertical placement whose degree belongs to the observed arrangement.
  - 88:19 **نُصِبَتْ** ن ص ب B001: Erecting something upright and prominent supplies fixed positioning within the terrestrial order.
  - 88:20 **سُطِحَتْ** س ط ح B001: An even extended surface supplies horizontal extent as the final dimension of the calibration sequence.

## jurisdictional handoff
- reading: علينا closes a division of offices: the messenger recalls without controlling, while God alone assumes oversight, compulsion, and final accountability.
- mechanism: The addressee is restricted to reminding and explicitly denied controlling surveillance. The next علينا therefore becomes a jurisdictional boundary: reminder remains human speech, while coercive oversight and حساب remain God's responsibility.
- trace:
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B006: Supervision and accountable management supplies the jurisdiction reserved by علينا after control is denied to the messenger.
  - 88:21 **فَذَكِّرْ مُذَكِّرٌ** ذ ك ر B009: Reminder as that which causes recollection supplies the messenger's limited communicative office.
  - 88:22 **لَّسْتَ** ل ي س B001: Present-state negation supplies the explicit exclusion of controlling authority from that office.
  - 88:22 **بِمُصَيْطِرٍ** س ط ر B003: The controlling overseer supplies the precise role denied to the messenger and reassigned at the level of divine حساب.

## evasion reversed into exposure
- reading: Reckoning actively reverses evasion: what was turned from and covered is brought back into presence, exposed, and made answerable.
- mechanism: Turning away and covering truth are followed by return. The information-testing branch of حساب becomes a reversal mechanism: evasion and concealment end in compelled presence, where the concealed matter is exposed and tested.
- trace:
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B010: Seeking and testing information supplies the inquiry that turns concealed conduct into examinable material.
  - 88:23 **تَوَلَّىٰ** و ل ي B007: Turning the back and withdrawing supplies the attempted movement away from address and accountability.
  - 88:23 **وَكَفَرَ** ك ف ر B003: Concealing truth supplies the hidden content that the account must make answerable.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return to the place of return supplies the spatial reversal that brings the one who withdrew back into presence.

## account after announced consequence
- reading: The account can be the comprehensive settling and administration of accountability after return, not merely the fact-finding stage before a sentence.
- mechanism: The discourse announces the greater punishment before it states return and then حساب. That order weakens a simple model in which حساب is preliminary evidence-gathering before any consequence; it permits حساب as comprehensive settlement and accountable custody that remains after the consequence has already been announced.
- trace:
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B006: Accountable management supplies a post-return settlement broad enough to follow an already announced consequence.
  - 88:24 **فَيُعَذِّبُهُ ٱلْعَذَابَ** ع ذ ب B005: Pain and punitive consequence supplies the sanction stated before the return-and-account pair.
  - 88:24 **ٱلْأَكْبَرَ** ك ب ر B002: The gravest or main matter supplies the announced consequence's enlarged scope.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return to the destination supplies the stage placed between announced punishment and undertaken account.

## return then undertaking
- reading: Return delivers them to the destination; then God explicitly assumes the distinct work and responsibility of their account.
- mechanism: Two nearly matched emphatic clauses separate destination from responsibility: their return is to Us, then their account is upon Us. The prepositional shift creates a two-stage mechanism of recovered presence followed by assumed undertaking.
- trace:
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B001: Counting and reckoning supplies the procedure that begins once return has delivered its subjects.
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B006: Accountable management supplies the obligation encoded by upon Us and distinguishes responsibility from destination.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B001: Return to the place of return supplies the first-stage movement into divine presence.

## celestial return cycle
- reading: Return and account faintly resemble completion of an ordered orbit: each life comes back into the measure governing its course.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B001: Counting and reckoning, whose packet scope includes ordered celestial courses, supplies the image of a completed and measurable cycle.
  - 88:25 **إِيَابَهُمْ** ء و ب B007: The sun setting toward its place of return supplies a cyclical model for إياب immediately before the final حساب.
  - 88:18 **ٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B004: The sky and whatever rises overhead supplies the visible field in which the return-cycle analogy operates.

## account as propping and placement
- reading: As a contained material analogy, the account props and places each subject into the condition that the reckoning supports.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B008: Cushioning and propping supplies the odd material focus image of an account that establishes a supported final position.
  - 88:13 **سُرُرٌ** س ر ر B011: A place of settling and reclining supplies the destination into which the propping image can place its subject.
  - 88:14 **مَّوْضُوعَةٌ** و ض ع B001: Putting something in an appointed position supplies the transition from abstract account to situated outcome.
  - 88:15 **مَصْفُوفَةٌ** ص ف ف B001: Alignment on an even line supplies ordered relation among the positions rather than arbitrary seating.

## account visible on faces
- reading: As a visual resonance, the account becomes publicly legible in the altered condition of the persons it differentiates.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B009: Mottled whiteness or altered skin coloring supplies the focus image of an inward account becoming bodily legible.
  - 88:1 **ٱلْغَٰشِيَةِ** غ ش و B007: Whiteness covering an animal's face supplies a second remote image of condition appearing across a face.
  - 88:2 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B001: The face as the forward visible surface supplies the first display site for the negative outcome.
  - 88:8 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B001: The repeated face supplies the matching display site on which the opposed positive outcome appears.

## social rank revalued
- reading: The account also revalues standing itself, deciding which merits truly count and reversing merely inherited or displayed prestige.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 88:26 **حِسَابَهُم** ح س ب B004: Nobility and counted merits supplies the focus question of what grounds of social worth are finally allowed to count.
  - 88:2 **وُجُوهٌ** و ج ه B006: Prestige and social standing supplies a status value that the humbled face destabilizes.
  - 88:10 **عَالِيَةٍ** ع ل و B002: Elevation in rank and honor supplies a positive status that can be distinguished from mere worldly standing.
  - 88:13 **مَّرْفُوعَةٌ** ر ف ع B002: Elevation of worth supplies the resulting reassignment of rank after what counts has been revalued.
